`lib/scoring/dates.ts`:

```ts
export function todayLondon(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: "Europe/London", year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit" }).format(now);
}

export function daysUntil(date: string, today: string): number {
  return Math.round((Date.parse(`${date}T00:00:00Z`) - Date.parse(`${today}T00:00:00Z`)) / 86_400_000);
}

export function isOpen(deadline: string | null, today: string): boolean {
  return deadline === null || daysUntil(deadline, today) >= 0;
}

export function deadlineLabel(deadline: string | null, today: string): string {
  if (!deadline) return "Rolling deadline";
  const d = daysUntil(deadline, today);
  if (d < 0) return "Closed";
  if (d === 0) return "Closes today";
  if (d === 1) return "Closes tomorrow";
  return `${d} days left`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement fit**

`lib/scoring/fit.ts`:

```ts
import type { Archetype, FitLabel, JobForScoring, Region, StudentProfile, Year } from "@/lib/types";
import { classifyRegion } from "@/lib/ingest/filter";
import type { CoverageResult } from "./coverage";
import { daysUntil } from "./dates";

export const FIT_WEIGHTS = { archetype: 0.35, coverage: 0.25, location: 0.15, accessibility: 0.25 } as const;
const GATE_CAP = 2.5;
const SOFT_FLAG_PENALTY = 0.5;

const ADJACENT: [Archetype, Archetype][] = [
  ["software_eng", "data_analytics"], ["data_analytics", "quant_trading"], ["quant_trading", "finance_banking"],
  ["finance_banking", "consulting"], ["consulting", "product"], ["product", "software_eng"],
  ["marketing_growth", "product"], ["research", "data_analytics"], ["research", "quant_trading"],
];
const isAdjacent = (a: Archetype, b: Archetype) => ADJACENT.some(([x, y]) => (x === a && y === b) || (x === b && y === a));

const YEAR_LABEL: Record<Year, string> = { "1st": "1st year", "2nd": "2nd year", penultimate: "penultimate year", final: "final year", masters: "masters" };

export type Gate = { key: "year" | "permanent" | "location" | "visa"; passed: boolean; reason?: string };
export type FitResult = {
  global: number; label: FitLabel; gates: Gate[]; capped: boolean;
  sub: { archetype: number; coverage: number; location: number; accessibility: number }; softFlags: string[];
};

export function labelFor(g: number): FitLabel {
  if (g >= 4.5) return "Strong fit";
  if (g >= 4.0) return "Good fit";
  if (g >= 3.5) return "Stretch";
  return "Not recommended";
}

function archetypeSub(student: StudentProfile, job: JobForScoring): number {
  if (!job.archetype || student.archetypes.length === 0) return 3;
  const keys = student.archetypes.map((a) => a.key);
  if (keys[0] === job.archetype) return 5;
  if (keys.slice(0, 2).includes(job.archetype)) return 4;
  if (keys.slice(0, 2).some((k) => isAdjacent(k, job.archetype!))) return 3;
  return 1.5;
}

function locationSub(region: Region, job: JobForScoring, prefs: Region[]): number {
  if (prefs.length === 0) return 4;
  if (prefs.includes(region)) return 5;
  if ((job.remote || region === "remote") && prefs.includes("remote")) return 5;
  if (region === "london" && prefs.includes("uk")) return 5;
  if (region === "uk" && prefs.includes("london")) return 3.5;
  if (job.remote || region === "remote") return 4;
  if (/hybrid/i.test(job.location)) return 3;
  return 2;
}

function accessibilitySub(student: StudentProfile, job: JobForScoring, today: string): number {
  let s: number;
  if (!job.deadline) s = 3.5;
  else {
    const d = daysUntil(job.deadline, today);
    s = d < 0 ? 1 : d <= 3 ? 3 : d <= 30 ? 4.5 : 4;
  }
  if (job.postedAt && daysUntil(today, job.postedAt.slice(0, 10)) > 60) s -= 0.5;
  if (job.firstYearFriendly && (student.year === "1st" || student.year === "2nd")) s += 0.5;
  return Math.min(5, Math.max(1, s));
}

export function scoreFit(student: StudentProfile, job: JobForScoring, coverage: CoverageResult, today: string): FitResult {
  const region = classifyRegion(job.location, job.remote);
  const loc = locationSub(region, job, student.locationPref);
  const gates: Gate[] = [
    job.yearEligibility.length > 0 && !job.yearEligibility.includes(student.year)
      ? { key: "year", passed: false, reason: `Open to ${job.yearEligibility.map((y) => YEAR_LABEL[y]).join(" / ")} only` }
      : { key: "year", passed: true },
    job.employmentType === "permanent"
      ? { key: "permanent", passed: false, reason: "Permanent full-time role" }
      : { key: "permanent", passed: true },
    loc <= 2 ? { key: "location", passed: false, reason: `Outside your locations (${job.location || "unknown"})` } : { key: "location", passed: true },
    student.needsVisa && job.sponsorsVisa === "no"
      ? { key: "visa", passed: false, reason: "No visa sponsorship" }
      : { key: "visa", passed: true },
  ];
  const softFlags: string[] = [];
  if (job.postedAt && daysUntil(today, job.postedAt.slice(0, 10)) > 90) softFlags.push("Posted over 90 days ago");

  const sub = {
    archetype: archetypeSub(student, job),
    coverage: coverage.weighted === null ? 3 : 1 + 4 * coverage.weighted,
    location: loc,
    accessibility: accessibilitySub(student, job, today),
  };
  let g = FIT_WEIGHTS.archetype * sub.archetype + FIT_WEIGHTS.coverage * sub.coverage +
    FIT_WEIGHTS.location * sub.location + FIT_WEIGHTS.accessibility * sub.accessibility - SOFT_FLAG_PENALTY * softFlags.length;
  g = Math.min(5, Math.max(1, g));
  const capped = gates.some((x) => !x.passed);
  if (capped) g = Math.min(g, GATE_CAP);
  const global = Math.round((g + 1e-9) * 10) / 10; // epsilon: 4.655 must round to 4.7, not 4.6
  return { global, label: labelFor(global), gates, capped, sub, softFlags };
}
```

```ts
import type { FitLabel } from "@/lib/types";
import { daysUntil } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";

export type XpEventType = "onboarding_complete" | "cv_uploaded" | "quiz_done" | "job_saved" | "cv_tailored" | "applied" | "interview" | "rejected" | "offer";
export type BadgeKey = "profile_pro" | "first_shot" | "spring_week_hunter" | "early_bird" | "bounce_back";

export const BADGES: Record<BadgeKey, { name: string; description: string }> = {
  profile_pro: { name: "Profile Pro", description: "Built your profile from a CV or the quiz" },
  first_shot: { name: "First Shot", description: "Sent your first application" },
  spring_week_hunter: { name: "Spring Week Hunter", description: "Applied to a spring week or insight programme" },
  early_bird: { name: "Early Bird", description: "Applied more than 7 days before the deadline" },
  bounce_back: { name: "Bounce Back", description: "Applied again after a rejection" },
};

export const LEVEL_NAMES = ["Explorer", "Applicant", "Contender", "Shortlisted", "Placed"] as const;

export type GamifyState = { xp: number; streakDays: number; lastActiveDate: string | null; badges: BadgeKey[]; savesToday: number; appliedCount: number; rejectedCount: number };
export type GamifyEvent = { type: XpEventType; fitLabel?: FitLabel; jobTitle?: string; deadline?: string | null; profileComplete?: boolean };
export type AwardResult = { xpGained: number; newXp: number; level: number; levelName: string; levelUp: boolean; streakDays: number; newBadges: BadgeKey[] };

export function levelFromXp(xp: number) {
  let level = 1, rem = xp;
  while (rem >= 100 * level) { rem -= 100 * level; level++; }
  return { level, name: LEVEL_NAMES[Math.min(level - 1, LEVEL_NAMES.length - 1)], intoLevel: rem, needed: 100 * level };
}

const APPLY_XP: Record<FitLabel, number> = { "Strong fit": 50, "Good fit": 50, Stretch: 25, "Not recommended": 10 };

export function xpFor(e: GamifyEvent, s: GamifyState): number {
  switch (e.type) {
    case "onboarding_complete": return 50;
    case "cv_uploaded": case "quiz_done": return 100;
    case "job_saved": return s.savesToday < 10 ? 5 : 0;
    case "cv_tailored": return 30;
    case "applied": return e.fitLabel ? APPLY_XP[e.fitLabel] : 25;
    case "interview": return 100;
    case "rejected": return 20;
    case "offer": return 300;
  }
}

export function computeAward(state: GamifyState, e: GamifyEvent, today: string) {
  const s = { ...state, savesToday: state.lastActiveDate === today ? state.savesToday : 0 };
  const xpGained = xpFor(e, s);
  const before = levelFromXp(s.xp);
  const after = levelFromXp(s.xp + xpGained);
  const streakDays = s.lastActiveDate === today ? Math.max(1, s.streakDays)
    : s.lastActiveDate && daysUntil(today, s.lastActiveDate) === 1 ? s.streakDays + 1 : 1;

  const earned = new Set(s.badges);
  const candidates: BadgeKey[] = [];
  if ((e.type === "cv_uploaded" || e.type === "quiz_done") && e.profileComplete) candidates.push("profile_pro");
  if (e.type === "applied") {
    if (s.appliedCount === 0) candidates.push("first_shot");
    if (e.jobTitle && /spring|insight/i.test(e.jobTitle)) candidates.push("spring_week_hunter");
    if (e.deadline && daysUntil(e.deadline, today) > 7) candidates.push("early_bird");
    if (s.rejectedCount > 0) candidates.push("bounce_back");
  }
  const newBadges = candidates.filter((b) => !earned.has(b));

  const next: GamifyState = {
    xp: s.xp + xpGained, streakDays, lastActiveDate: today, badges: [...s.badges, ...newBadges],
    savesToday: s.savesToday + (e.type === "job_saved" ? 1 : 0),
    appliedCount: s.appliedCount + (e.type === "applied" ? 1 : 0),
    rejectedCount: s.rejectedCount + (e.type === "rejected" ? 1 : 0),
  };
  const result: AwardResult = {
    xpGained, newXp: next.xp, level: after.level, levelName: after.name, levelUp: after.level > before.level, streakDays, newBadges,
  };
  return { result, next };
}
```

```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import type { Archetype, ArchetypeScore, Region, StudentProfile, Year } from "@/lib/types";

export type ProfileRow = {
  user_id: string; display_name: string | null; year: Year | null; field: string | null; location_pref: Region[];
  needs_visa: boolean; target_archetype: Archetype | null; archetypes: ArchetypeScore[]; skills: string[];
  onboarded: boolean; xp: number; level: number; streak_days: number; last_active_date: string | null;
};

export async function getProfile(supabase: SupabaseClient, userId: string): Promise<ProfileRow> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("*").eq("user_id", userId).single();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data as ProfileRow;
}

export function toStudent(p: ProfileRow): StudentProfile {
  return {
    year: p.year ?? "2nd",
    locationPref: p.location_pref ?? [],
    needsVisa: p.needs_visa,
    archetypes: [...(p.archetypes ?? [])].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score),
    skills: p.skills ?? [],
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `awardXP` (server)**

`lib/gamify/award.ts`:

```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { todayLondon } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";
import { computeAward, type AwardResult, type BadgeKey, type GamifyEvent } from "./core";

const ONCE_PER_JOB = new Set(["job_saved", "cv_tailored", "applied", "interview", "rejected", "offer"]);

export async function awardXP(supabase: SupabaseClient, userId: string, e: GamifyEvent, jobId?: string): Promise<AwardResult> {
  const today = todayLondon();
  if (jobId && ONCE_PER_JOB.has(e.type)) {
    const { count } = await supabase.from("xp_events").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })
      .eq("user_id", userId).eq("type", e.type).eq("job_id", jobId);
    if ((count ?? 0) > 0) {
      const { data: p } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("xp, level, streak_days").eq("user_id", userId).single();
      return { xpGained: 0, newXp: p?.xp ?? 0, level: p?.level ?? 1, levelName: "", levelUp: false, streakDays: p?.streak_days ?? 0, newBadges: [] };
    }
  }
  const [{ data: p }, { data: badges }, { count: applied }, { count: rejected }, { count: saves }] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from("profiles").select("xp, streak_days, last_active_date").eq("user_id", userId).single(),
    supabase.from("badges").select("badge_key").eq("user_id", userId),
    supabase.from("xp_events").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("user_id", userId).eq("type", "applied"),
    supabase.from("xp_events").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("user_id", userId).eq("type", "rejected"),
    supabase.from("xp_events").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("user_id", userId).eq("type", "job_saved").gte("created_at", `${today}T00:00:00Z`),
  ]);
  const { result, next } = computeAward({
    xp: p?.xp ?? 0, streakDays: p?.streak_days ?? 0, lastActiveDate: p?.last_active_date ?? null,
    badges: (badges ?? []).map((b) => b.badge_key as BadgeKey),
    savesToday: saves ?? 0, appliedCount: applied ?? 0, rejectedCount: rejected ?? 0,
  }, e, today);

  await supabase.from("xp_events").insert({ user_id: userId, type: e.type, xp: result.xpGained, job_id: jobId ?? null });
  if (result.newBadges.length) await supabase.from("badges").insert(result.newBadges.map((badge_key) => ({ user_id: userId, badge_key })));
  await supabase.from("profiles").update({ xp: next.xp, level: result.level, streak_days: next.streakDays, last_active_date: today }).eq("user_id", userId);
  return result;
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import confetti from "canvas-confetti";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { BADGES, type AwardResult } from "@/lib/gamify/core";

function Star() {
  return (
    <svg width="28" height="28" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="#FFC800" stroke="#9A6B00" strokeWidth="1.5" aria-hidden="true">
      <path d="M12 2l3 6.5 7 .8-5.2 4.8 1.4 7L12 17.6 5.8 21l1.4-7L2 9.3l7-.8z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function celebrate(r: AwardResult | null) {
  if (!r || r.xpGained === 0) return;
  const badge = r.newBadges[0] ? BADGES[r.newBadges[0]] : null;
  toast.custom(() => (
    <div role="status" className="card-quest flex w-[340px] items-center gap-3.5 p-4 shadow-xl">
      <div className="flex h-13 w-13 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-[#FFF4D6]"><Star /></div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
        <div className="num-quest text-xl text-[#9A6B00]">+{r.xpGained} XP</div>
        <div className="text-sm font-bold">
          {badge ? `Badge unlocked: ${badge.name}` : r.levelUp ? `Level up! You're now a ${r.levelName}` : `${r.streakDays}-day streak`}
        </div>
        {badge && <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{badge.description}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  ), { duration: 4000 });
  if (r.levelUp || r.newBadges.length) {
    confetti({ particleCount: 120, spread: 70, origin: { y: 0.8 }, colors: ["#5B3DF5", "#FFC800", "#FF9600", "#0E8A5F"] });
  }
}
```

```tsx
}
```

`lib/validation/onboarding.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { YEARS } from "@/lib/types";

export const FIELDS = ["Economics", "Computer Science", "Mathematics", "Engineering", "Physics", "Management", "Finance & Accounting", "Statistics & Data Science", "Law", "Psychology", "Politics & IR", "History", "Natural Sciences", "Other"] as const;

export const OnboardingInput = z.object({
  year: z.enum(YEARS),
  field: z.string().min(1).max(80),
  locationPref: z.array(z.enum(["london", "uk", "europe", "remote"])).min(1),
  needsVisa: z.boolean(),
});
export type OnboardingInputT = z.infer<typeof OnboardingInput>;
```

`app/onboarding/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { awardXP } from "@/lib/gamify/award";
import { OnboardingInput, type OnboardingInputT } from "@/lib/validation/onboarding";

export async function saveOnboarding(input: OnboardingInputT) {
  const parsed = OnboardingInput.safeParse(input);
  if (!parsed.success) return { error: "Please answer every step." };
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const { data: before } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("onboarded").eq("user_id", user.id).single();
  const { error } = await supabase.from("profiles").update({
    year: parsed.data.year, field: parsed.data.field, location_pref: parsed.data.locationPref,
    needs_visa: parsed.data.needsVisa, onboarded: true,
  }).eq("user_id", user.id);
  if (error) return { error: "Couldn't save — try again." };
  const award = before?.onboarded ? null : await awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: "onboarding_complete" });
  return { award };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Onboarding page (tap-only, 3 steps)**

`app/onboarding/page.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Progress } from "@/components/ui/progress";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { YEARS, type Year } from "@/lib/types";
import { FIELDS } from "@/lib/validation/onboarding";
import { celebrate } from "@/components/reward-toast";
import { saveOnboarding } from "./actions";

const YEAR_LABEL: Record<Year, string> = { "1st": "1st year", "2nd": "2nd year", penultimate: "Penultimate", final: "Final year", masters: "Master's" };
const LOCATIONS = [["london", "London"], ["uk", "Anywhere in the UK"], ["europe", "Europe"], ["remote", "Remote"]] as const;

function Choice({ selected, onClick, children }: { selected: boolean; onClick: () => void; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" onClick={onClick} aria-pressed={selected}
      className={`card-quest min-h-14 px-4 py-3 text-left font-bold transition ${selected ? "border-primary bg-[#EFEBFF] text-[#3A20C4]" : "hover:border-[#C9C2FF]"}`}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

export default function Onboarding() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [year, setYear] = useState<Year | null>(null);
  const [field, setField] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loc, setLoc] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [visa, setVisa] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const canNext = [year, field, loc.length > 0][step];

  function finish() {
    start(async () => {
      const r = await saveOnboarding({ year: year!, field: field!, locationPref: loc as never, needsVisa: visa });
      if ("error" in r) { setError(r.error ?? "Something went wrong"); return; }
      celebrate(r.award);
      router.push("/profile/build");
    });
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto flex min-h-dvh max-w-xl flex-col gap-6 px-6 py-10">
      <Progress value={((step + 1) / 3) * 100} />
      {step === 0 && (<section className="flex flex-col gap-3"><h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold">What year are you in?</h1>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">{YEARS.map((y) => <Choice key={y} selected={year === y} onClick={() => setYear(y)}>{YEAR_LABEL[y]}</Choice>)}</div></section>)}
      {step === 1 && (<section className="flex flex-col gap-3"><h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold">What do you study?</h1>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">{FIELDS.map((f) => <Choice key={f} selected={field === f} onClick={() => setField(f)}>{f}</Choice>)}</div></section>)}
      {step === 2 && (<section className="flex flex-col gap-3"><h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold">Where do you want to work?</h1>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">{LOCATIONS.map(([k, label]) => (
          <Choice key={k} selected={loc.includes(k)} onClick={() => setLoc((l) => l.includes(k) ? l.filter((x) => x !== k) : [...l, k])}>{label}</Choice>))}</div>
        <label className="card-quest flex min-h-14 items-center justify-between px-4 font-bold">I&apos;ll need visa sponsorship <Switch checked={visa} onCheckedChange={setVisa} /></label></section>)}
      {error && <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>}
      <div className="flex gap-3">
        {step > 0 && <button type="button" onClick={() => setStep(step - 1)} className="rounded-[14px] border-2 px-5 py-3 font-bold">Back</button>}
        <button type="button" disabled={!canNext || pending} onClick={() => (step < 2 ? setStep(step + 1) : finish())} className="btn-quest flex-1 py-3 disabled:opacity-50">
          {step < 2 ? "Next" : pending ? "Saving…" : "Let's go"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

```ts
Python, SQL, Excel
INTERESTS
Running (London 10k 2025), chess`;

    expect(cv.skills.flatMap((g) => g.items)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["Python", "SQL", "Excel"]));
    expect(cv.interests).toContain("Running");
  });
});

describe("isImageOnly", () => {
  it("flags pages with almost no text", () => {
    expect(isImageOnly(["", "  "])).toBe(true);
    expect(isImageOnly(["a".repeat(800)])).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: PDF text + ids**

`lib/cv/pdf-text.ts`:

```ts
import { extractText, getDocumentProxy } from "unpdf";

export async function extractPdf(bytes: Uint8Array) {
  const pdf = await getDocumentProxy(bytes);
  const { totalPages, text } = await extractText(pdf, { mergePages: false });
  const pageTexts = Array.isArray(text) ? text : [text];
  return { text: pageTexts.join("\n"), pageTexts, pageCount: totalPages };
}

export function isImageOnly(pageTexts: string[]): boolean {
  const chars = pageTexts.reduce((n, p) => n + p.replace(/\s/g, "").length, 0);
  return chars / Math.max(1, pageTexts.length) < 200;
}
```

`lib/cv/ids.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, CvEntry } from "@/lib/types";

const entries = (list: CvEntry[], p: string): CvEntry[] =>
  list.map((e, i) => ({ ...e, id: `${p}${i}`, bullets: e.bullets.map((b, j) => ({ ...b, id: `${p}${i}-b${j}` })) }));

export function assignIds(cv: ParsedCV): ParsedCV {
  return {
    ...cv,
    education: cv.education.map((e, i) => ({ ...e, id: `e${i}`, details: e.details.map((d, j) => ({ ...d, id: `e${i}-d${j}` })) })),
    experience: entries(cv.experience, "x"),
    projects: entries(cv.projects, "p"),
    leadership: entries(cv.leadership, "l"),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Heuristic parser (append to `lib/ai/heuristics.ts`)**

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, CvEntry } from "@/lib/types";

const SECTION_RE: [string, RegExp][] = [
  ["education", /^(education|academic background)$/i],
  ["experience", /^(experience|work experience|professional experience|employment)$/i],
  ["projects", /^(projects|personal projects|technical projects)$/i],
  ["leadership", /^(leadership|activities|extracurricular( activities)?|positions of responsibility|leadership (&|and) activities)$/i],
  ["skills", /^(skills|technical skills|skills (&|and) interests)$/i],
  ["interests", /^(interests|hobbies|interests (&|and) hobbies)$/i],
  ["summary", /^(profile|summary|personal statement|about me)$/i],
];
const BULLET = /^\s*([•▪◦·*\-–]|\d+\.)\s+/;

export function heuristicParseCv(text: string): ParsedCV {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const email = text.match(/[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.-]+/)?.[0] ?? null;
  const phone = text.match(/(\+?\d[\d\s()-]{8,}\d)/)?.[0]?.trim() ?? null;
  const links = [...text.matchAll(/\b((?:https?:\/\/)?(?:www\.)?(?:github\.com|gitlab\.com|linkedin\.com\/in)\/[\w-]+)/gi)].map((m) => m[1]);
  const sections: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  const order: string[] = [];
  let current = "header";
  for (const line of lines) {
    const hit = SECTION_RE.find(([, re]) => re.test(line.replace(/[:\s]+$/, "")));
    if (hit) { current = hit[0]; if (!order.includes(current)) order.push(current); sections[current] = []; continue; }
    (sections[current] ??= []).push(line);
  }
  const toEntries = (ls: string[] = []): CvEntry[] => {
    const out: CvEntry[] = [];
    for (const l of ls) {
      if (BULLET.test(l)) {
        if (!out.length) out.push({ id: "", org: "", title: "", dates: "", location: "", bullets: [] });
        out[out.length - 1].bullets.push({ id: "", text: l.replace(BULLET, "") });
      } else {
        const [head, ...rest] = l.split(/\s+[—–-]\s+|,\s+(?=\d{4})/);
        const dates = l.match(/(\d{4}\s*[–-]\s*(\d{4}|present))/i)?.[1] ?? "";
        out.push({ id: "", org: head ?? l, title: rest.join(", ").replace(dates, "").replace(/,\s*$/, "").trim(), dates, location: "", bullets: [] });
      }
    }
    return out;
  };
  const edu = (sections.education ?? []);
  const skillItems = (sections.skills ?? []).join(", ").split(/[,;|]/).map((s) => s.replace(/^.*?:\s*/, "").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return {
    name: lines[0] ?? "",
    email, phone, location: /\blondon\b/i.test(lines.slice(0, 3).join(" ")) ? "London" : null, links,
    summary: sections.summary?.join(" ") ?? null,
    education: edu.length ? [{
      id: "", institution: edu[0].split(/\s+[—–-]\s+/)[0], degree: edu[0].split(/\s+[—–-]\s+/)[1] ?? "",
      dates: edu[0].match(/\d{4}\s*[–-]\s*\d{4}/)?.[0] ?? "",
      grade: edu.find((l) => /(2:1|2:2|first|predicted|\d{2}%|a\*|aaa)/i.test(l)) ?? null,
      details: edu.slice(1).map((t) => ({ id: "", text: t.replace(BULLET, "") })),
    }] : [],
    experience: toEntries(sections.experience),
    projects: toEntries(sections.projects),
    leadership: toEntries(sections.leadership),
    skills: skillItems.length ? [{ group: "Skills", items: skillItems }] : [],
    interests: sections.interests?.join(" ") ?? null,
    sectionOrder: order.filter((s) => s !== "summary"),
    yearGuess: null,
    archetypes: archetypeScores(text).slice(0, 3),
  };
}
```

```ts
const Bullet = z.object({ id: z.string(), text: z.string() });
const Entry = z.object({ id: z.string(), org: z.string(), title: z.string(), dates: z.string(), location: z.string(), bullets: z.array(Bullet) });
export const ArchetypeScoreOut = z.object({ key: z.enum(ARCHETYPES), score: z.number(), why: z.array(z.string()) });

export const ParsedCvOutput = z.object({
  name: z.string(), email: z.string().nullable(), phone: z.string().nullable(), location: z.string().nullable(),
  links: z.array(z.string()), summary: z.string().nullable(),
  education: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string(), institution: z.string(), degree: z.string(), dates: z.string(), grade: z.string().nullable(), details: z.array(Bullet) })),
  experience: z.array(Entry), projects: z.array(Entry), leadership: z.array(Entry),
  skills: z.array(z.object({ group: z.string(), items: z.array(z.string()) })),
  interests: z.string().nullable(), sectionOrder: z.array(z.string()),
  yearGuess: z.enum(YEARS).nullable(), archetypes: z.array(ArchetypeScoreOut),
});
```

Append to `lib/ai/prompts.ts`:

```ts
export const SYSTEM_PARSE_CV = `You convert a UK student's CV (PDF) into structured JSON for a careers app.
- Copy every bullet point VERBATIM (fix nothing, add nothing). Never invent employers, dates, numbers or skills.
- experience = paid/unpaid roles; leadership = societies, positions of responsibility, volunteering; projects = personal/academic projects.
- sectionOrder lists the CV's sections in the order they appear, using: education, experience, projects, leadership, skills, interests.
- yearGuess from graduation dates relative to October 2026 (graduating 2029 → "1st", 2028 → "2nd", 2027 → "final"; masters if a postgraduate degree is in progress), else null.
- archetypes: the 3 best-fitting from the allowed list with score 0-100 and 1-3 "why" lines that each cite something actually in the CV.
- Use empty strings/arrays or null when something is missing. ids can be any placeholder; they are reassigned.`;
```

`lib/ai/parseCV.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV } from "@/lib/types";
import { assignIds } from "@/lib/cv/ids";
import { generateStructured, runAi } from "./provider";
import { SYSTEM_PARSE_CV } from "./prompts";
import { ParsedCvOutput } from "./schemas";
import { heuristicParseCv } from "./heuristics";

export async function parseCV(input: { base64: string; text: string; fixtureKey?: string }): Promise<ParsedCV> {
  const cv = await runAi({
    fn: "parseCV",
    fixtureKey: input.fixtureKey,
    schema: ParsedCvOutput,
    live: () => generateStructured({
      tier: "smart", system: SYSTEM_PARSE_CV, schema: ParsedCvOutput, maxTokens: 16000,
      parts: [{ type: "pdf", base64: input.base64 }, { type: "text", text: "Parse this CV." }],
    }),
    fallback: () => heuristicParseCv(input.text),
  });
  return assignIds(cv as ParsedCV);
}
```

No `fixtures/ai/parseCV/default.json` is created: in fixture mode the heuristic parser is the default, and only the demo persona gets an exact fixture (Task 25).

- [ ] **Step 6: Upload action**

`app/(app)/profile/build/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { extractPdf, isImageOnly } from "@/lib/cv/pdf-text";
import { parseCV } from "@/lib/ai/parseCV";
import { awardXP } from "@/lib/gamify/award";
import { contentHash } from "@/lib/ingest/fingerprint";
import { canonicalize, extractSkills } from "@/lib/skills/extract";

const MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

export async function uploadCv(formData: FormData) {
  const file = formData.get("cv");
  if (!(file instanceof File)) return { error: "Choose a PDF first." };
  if (file.type !== "application/pdf") return { error: "Please upload a PDF." };
  if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) return { error: "That file is over 5 MB — export a smaller PDF." };

  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
  let pdf;
  try { pdf = await extractPdf(bytes.slice()); } catch { return { error: "We couldn't open this PDF. Try exporting it again, or take the quiz instead." }; }
  const base64 = Buffer.from(bytes).toString("base64");
  const imageOnly = isImageOnly(pdf.pageTexts);

  let parsed;
  try { parsed = await parseCV({ base64, text: pdf.text, fixtureKey: contentHash(base64).slice(0, 12) }); }
  catch { return { error: "We couldn't read this CV right now. Try again in a minute, or take the quiz." }; }

  const admin = createAdminClient();
  const path = `${user.id}/${Date.now()}.pdf`;
  const up = await admin.storage.from("cvs").upload(path, bytes, { contentType: "application/pdf" });
  if (up.error) return { error: "Upload failed — try again." };

  await supabase.from("cvs").update({ is_active: false }).eq("user_id", user.id);
  const { data: cvRow, error } = await supabase.from("cvs").insert({
    user_id: user.id, storage_path: path, file_size: file.size, page_count: pdf.pageCount,
    raw_text: pdf.text, page_texts: pdf.pageTexts, parsed_json: parsed, is_active: true,
  }).select("id").single();
  if (error || !cvRow) return { error: "Couldn't save your CV — try again." };

  const skills = [...new Set([...parsed.skills.flatMap((g) => g.items.map((i) => canonicalize(i) ?? i)), ...extractSkills(pdf.text)])];
  const { data: prof } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("year, target_archetype").eq("user_id", user.id).single();
  await supabase.from("profiles").update({
    archetypes: parsed.archetypes, skills,
    target_archetype: prof?.target_archetype ?? parsed.archetypes[0]?.key ?? null,
    year: prof?.year ?? parsed.yearGuess ?? null,
  }).eq("user_id", user.id);

  const award = await awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: "cv_uploaded", profileComplete: true });
  return { ok: true as const, award, imageOnly, cvId: cvRow.id as string };
}
```

`app/(app)/profile/build/upload-card.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { celebrate } from "@/components/reward-toast";
import { uploadCv } from "./actions";

const STEPS = ["Reading your CV…", "Spotting your skills…", "Finding your roles…", "Scoring against recruiter rules…"];

export function UploadCard() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pending) { setStep(0); return; }
    const t = setInterval(() => setStep((s) => Math.min(s + 1, STEPS.length - 1)), 1800);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [pending]);

  return (
    <form action={(fd) => start(async () => {
      setError(null);
      const r = await uploadCv(fd);
      if ("error" in r) { setError(r.error ?? "Upload failed"); return; }
      celebrate(r.award);
      router.push(r.imageOnly ? "/profile?warn=image" : "/profile");
    })} className="card-quest flex flex-col gap-4 p-6">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold">Upload your CV</h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">PDF, up to 5 MB. We read it, score it against recruiter guidance, and never share it.</p>
      <label className="flex min-h-36 cursor-pointer flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#C9C2FF] bg-[#F8F7FF] p-6 text-center">
        <span className="font-bold">Drop your PDF here or click to choose</span>
        <input type="file" name="cv" accept="application/pdf" required className="sr-only" onChange={(e) => e.currentTarget.form?.requestSubmit()} />
      </label>
      {pending && <p role="status" className="font-semibold text-[#3A20C4]">{STEPS[step]}</p>}
      {error && <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>}
    </form>
  );
}
```

### Task 16: CV rule library (161 cited rules as data)

**Files:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Types and checker ids**

`lib/cv-rules/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { Archetype, Year } from "@/lib/types";

export type Dimension = "ats" | "impact" | "clarity" | "relevance" | "uk";
export type Severity = "critical" | "major" | "minor";
export type Evidence = "A" | "B" | "C" | "D";

export const CHECKER_IDS = [
  "text_layer", "file_size", "page_count", "section_headings", "education_first", "contact_present",
  "uk_personal_details", "quantified_bullets", "we_vs_i", "skill_rating_glyphs", "uk_grades", "github_link",
  "outdated_tools", "skills_count", "bullet_length", "summary_length", "interests_present", "generic_interests",
  "leadership_lexicon", "projects_count", "project_stack", "spelling",
] as const;
export type CheckerId = (typeof CHECKER_IDS)[number];

export type Citation = { title: string; publisher: string; year: string; url: string };

export type CvRule = {
  id: string;
  sectors: Archetype[] | "all";
  dimension: Dimension;
  statement: string;
  check: "code" | "llm";
  checker?: CheckerId;
  params?: { maxPages?: number; threshold?: number };
  severity: Severity;
  evidence: Evidence;
  citations: Citation[];
  yearAdjust?: Partial<Record<Year, { severity?: Severity; threshold?: number; skip?: boolean }>>;
  llmGuidance?: string;
};
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { RULES, rulesFor, DIMENSION_WEIGHTS, MYTHS } from "@/lib/cv-rules";
import { CHECKER_IDS } from "@/lib/cv-rules/types";
import { ARCHETYPES } from "@/lib/types";

describe("CV rule library", () => {
  it("has the transcribed research rules with unique ids", () => {
    expect(RULES.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(150);
    expect(new Set(RULES.map((r) => r.id)).size).toBe(RULES.length);
  });
  it("every non-D rule cites at least one source with a URL", () => {
    for (const r of RULES.filter((r) => r.evidence !== "D")) {
      expect(r.citations.length, r.id).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      for (const c of r.citations) expect(c.url, r.id).toMatch(/^https?:\/\//);
    }
  });
  it("code rules point at a real checker; llm rules have guidance", () => {
    for (const r of RULES) {
      if (r.check === "code") expect(CHECKER_IDS, r.id).toContain(r.checker);
      else expect(r.statement.length + (r.llmGuidance?.length ?? 0), r.id).toBeGreaterThan(20);
    }
  });
  it("every sector gets at least 25 applicable rules", () => {
    for (const a of ARCHETYPES) expect(rulesFor(a).length, a).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(25);
  });
  it("sector weights sum to 100", () => {
    for (const a of ARCHETYPES) expect(Object.values(DIMENSION_WEIGHTS[a]).reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0), a).toBe(100);
  });
  it("keeps the 11 debunked myths", () => {
    expect(MYTHS).toHaveLength(11);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Transcribe the rules**

Create `lib/cv-rules/rules.ts` exporting `RULES: CvRule[]`, transcribing **every** rule from:
- `docs/research/cv-rules-general.md` (G-01…G-37) → `sectors: "all"`
- `docs/research/cv-rules-finance-consulting.md` (shared FCQ rules → `["finance_banking","quant_trading","consulting"]`; FIN → `["finance_banking"]`; QNT → `["quant_trading"]`; CON → `["consulting"]`)
- `docs/research/cv-rules-tech-product.md` (shared rules → `["software_eng","data_analytics","product","marketing_growth","research"]`; SWE/DAT/PRD/MKT/RES → their archetype)

Mapping rules for each field:
- `id`: keep the doc's id exactly (e.g. `G-01`, `FIN-04`).
- `statement`: one or two plain sentences written for a student.
- `evidence`: strong → A, moderate → B, weak → C, consensus-only → D. Where a doc already uses A–D, keep it; if it gives two grades ("A (2:1) / C (UCAS figure)"), use the first.
- `severity`: copy it. If it is year-dependent ("major (penultimate); minor (1st year)"), set the base value and put the rest in `yearAdjust`.
- `dimension`:
  - parse, format or file → `ats`
  - outcomes, leadership, commitment, depth → `impact`
  - length, style, typos, consistency → `clarity`
  - skills, keywords, projects, sector evidence → `relevance`
  - grades, personal details, UK format → `uk`
- `check`: `"code"` only if the doc says RULE **and** a checker below covers it; otherwise `"llm"` with `llmGuidance` taken from the doc's check text. Map RULE checks onto the checkers like this:

  | Checker id | Rules it covers |
  |---|---|
  | `text_layer` | image-only PDF |
  | `file_size` | file size |
  | `page_count` | length; `params.maxPages` from the rule (SWE/finance 1, general 2) |
  | `section_headings` | standard headings |
  | `education_first` | section order |
  | `contact_present` | phone/email |
  | `uk_personal_details` | photo/DOB/age/marital |
  | `quantified_bullets` | share of bullets with numbers; `params.threshold` and `yearAdjust` thresholds from the docs |
  | `we_vs_i` | we/I ratio |
  | `skill_rating_glyphs` | bars/stars/x/10 |
  | `uk_grades` | grade stated |
  | `github_link` | GitHub/portfolio |
  | `outdated_tools` | Universal Analytics etc. |
  | `skills_count` | curated skills count |
  | `bullet_length` | bullet length bands |
  | `summary_length` | profile too long |
  | `interests_present` | interests line |
  | `generic_interests` | generic interests |
  | `leadership_lexicon` | leadership evidence |
  | `projects_count` | 2–4 projects |
  | `project_stack` | tech stack per project |
  | `spelling` | typos (fed by the LLM's error list) |

- `citations`: resolve the doc's short refs (`[S3]`, numbered source lists) to `{ title, publisher, year, url }` from that doc's sources section. Include only sources that have a URL. A rule whose doc entry says "—"/no source is evidence `D` with `citations: []`.
- Rules the docs flag as unverified (PwC 2:1 date, "216 UCAS points", the LSE test-domain page, snippet-only Bright Network, Lever and iCIMS pages) stay in the library. Downgrade their evidence by one grade and add `" (unverified)"` to the citation title.

Example of the expected shape (these three must appear exactly):

```ts

export const RULES: CvRule[] = [
  {
    id: "G-01", sectors: "all", dimension: "ats", check: "code", checker: "text_layer", severity: "critical", evidence: "A",
    statement: "Submit a text-based PDF or DOCX, never an image or a scanned page — ATS can't read images.",
    citations: [
      { title: "Unsuccessful resume parse", publisher: "Greenhouse Support", year: "current", url: "https://support.greenhouse.io/hc/en-us/articles/200989175-Unsuccessful-resume-parse" },
      { title: "Resume parsing", publisher: "Workday Admin Guide", year: "current", url: "https://doc.workday.com/admin-guide/en-us/human-capital-management/recruiting/candidates/set-up-prospects-and-candidates/hdc1552497830785.html" },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: "SWE-01", sectors: ["software_eng"], dimension: "clarity", check: "code", checker: "page_count", params: { maxPages: 1 }, severity: "major", evidence: "A",
    statement: "Keep internship CVs to one full page; a page-and-a-bit spillover reads as unedited.",
    citations: [/* resolve [S2], [S6], [S3] from cv-rules-tech-product.md sources */],
  },
  {
    id: "FIN-04", sectors: ["finance_banking"], dimension: "impact", check: "llm", severity: "major", evidence: "B",

    llmGuidance: "Look in Activities/Experience for finance society roles (member vs committee vs organiser), spring weeks/insight days, virtual internships, stock-pitch or trading competitions, a personal portfolio. pass = committee-level or competition/insight evidence; partial = membership only; fail = none.",
    yearAdjust: { "1st": { severity: "minor" } },
    citations: [/* resolve [S9], [S7], [S1] from cv-rules-finance-consulting.md sources */],
```

The `/* resolve … */` comments are instructions for the transcriber: replace them with real `Citation` objects. The finished file contains no comments of that kind.

```ts
import type { Archetype } from "@/lib/types";
import { RULES } from "./rules";
export { RULES };
export { DIMENSION_WEIGHTS, BASE_WEIGHTS } from "./profiles";
export { MYTHS } from "./myths";
export * from "./types";

export const RULES_BY_ID = new Map(RULES.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
export function rulesFor(sector: Archetype) {
  return RULES.filter((r) => r.sectors === "all" || r.sectors.includes(sector));
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { CHECKERS, runCodeChecks } from "@/lib/cv-score/checkers";
import type { CvContext } from "@/lib/cv-score/context";
import type { CvRule } from "@/lib/cv-rules/types";

const cv = (over: Partial<ParsedCV> = {}): ParsedCV => ({

  education: [{ id: "e0", institution: "UCL", degree: "BSc Economics", dates: "2024–2027", grade: "Predicted 2:1", details: [] }],
  experience: [{ id: "x0", org: "Finance Society", title: "Events Officer", dates: "2025", location: "", bullets: [
    { id: "x0-b0", text: "Organised weekly society events" }, { id: "x0-b1", text: "Managed a budget of £2,000" }] }],
  projects: [], leadership: [], skills: [{ group: "Skills", items: ["Python", "SQL", "Excel", "Tableau", "R", "Stata", "PowerPoint", "VBA"] }],
  interests: "Running (London 10k 2025), chess", sectionOrder: ["education", "experience", "skills", "interests"], yearGuess: null, archetypes: [], ...over,
});
const ctx = (over: Partial<CvContext> = {}): CvContext => ({
  text: "x".repeat(900), pageTexts: ["x".repeat(900)], pageCount: 1, fileSize: 120_000, cv: cv(), year: "2nd", spellingErrors: [], ...over,
});
const rule = (over: Partial<CvRule>): CvRule => ({ id: "T", sectors: "all", dimension: "ats", statement: "s", check: "code", severity: "major", evidence: "A", citations: [], ...over });

describe("checkers", () => {
  it("text_layer fails image-only PDFs", () => {
    expect(CHECKERS.text_layer(ctx({ pageTexts: [""], text: "" }), rule({})).status).toBe("fail");
    expect(CHECKERS.text_layer(ctx(), rule({})).status).toBe("pass");
  });
  it("page_count respects maxPages and flags spillover as partial", () => {
    expect(CHECKERS.page_count(ctx({ pageCount: 2 }), rule({ params: { maxPages: 1 } })).status).toBe("partial");
    expect(CHECKERS.page_count(ctx({ pageCount: 3 }), rule({ params: { maxPages: 2 } })).status).toBe("fail");
  });
  it("quantified_bullets uses year-adjusted thresholds and reports the first unquantified bullet", () => {
    const r = rule({ params: { threshold: 0.4 }, yearAdjust: { "1st": { threshold: 0.2 } } });
    const out = CHECKERS.quantified_bullets(ctx(), r); // 1 of 2 = 50%
    expect(out.status).toBe("pass");
    const strict = CHECKERS.quantified_bullets(ctx(), rule({ params: { threshold: 0.8 } }));
    expect(strict.status).toBe("partial");
    expect(strict.evidenceLine).toBe("Organised weekly society events");

  it("uk_personal_details flags DOB/nationality", () => {

  });
  it("uk_grades passes with a stated classification", () => {
    expect(CHECKERS.uk_grades(ctx(), rule({})).status).toBe("pass");
    expect(CHECKERS.uk_grades(ctx({ cv: cv({ education: [{ id: "e0", institution: "UCL", degree: "BSc", dates: "", grade: null, details: [] }] }) }), rule({})).status).toBe("fail");
  });
  it("skill_rating_glyphs catches bars and x/10", () => {
    expect(CHECKERS.skill_rating_glyphs(ctx({ text: "Python ●●●●○  Excel 8/10" }), rule({})).status).toBe("fail");
  });
  it("spelling: na without data, fail at 3+", () => {
    expect(CHECKERS.spelling(ctx({ spellingErrors: null }), rule({})).status).toBe("na");
    expect(CHECKERS.spelling(ctx({ spellingErrors: ["teh", "recieve", "managment"] }), rule({})).status).toBe("fail");
  });
  it("generic_interests flags vague hobbies but accepts specific ones", () => {
    expect(CHECKERS.generic_interests(ctx({ cv: cv({ interests: "Reading, travelling, socialising" }) }), rule({})).status).toBe("fail");
    expect(CHECKERS.generic_interests(ctx(), rule({})).status).toBe("pass");
  });
  it("runCodeChecks only runs code rules and skips year-skipped rules", () => {

      rule({ id: "A2", check: "llm" }),

    ], ctx());
    expect(results.map((r) => r.ruleId)).toEqual(["A1", "A3"]);
    expect(results[1].status).toBe("na");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`lib/cv-score/context.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, Year } from "@/lib/types";

export type CvContext = { text: string; pageTexts: string[]; pageCount: number; fileSize: number; cv: ParsedCV; year: Year; spellingErrors: string[] | null };
export type RuleStatus = "pass" | "partial" | "fail" | "na";
export type RuleResult = { ruleId: string; status: RuleStatus; evidenceLine: string | null; fix: string | null };
```

`lib/cv-score/checkers.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, Year } from "@/lib/types";
import type { CheckerId, CvRule } from "@/lib/cv-rules/types";
import { extractSkills } from "@/lib/skills/extract";
import type { CvContext, RuleResult } from "./context";

type Out = Omit<RuleResult, "ruleId">;
const ok = (): Out => ({ status: "pass", evidenceLine: null, fix: null });
const na = (): Out => ({ status: "na", evidenceLine: null, fix: null });
const bad = (status: "fail" | "partial", evidenceLine: string | null, fix: string): Out => ({ status, evidenceLine, fix });

export function allBullets(cv: ParsedCV) {
  return [...cv.experience, ...cv.projects, ...cv.leadership].flatMap((e) => e.bullets);
}

export function thresholdFor(rule: CvRule, year: Year, fallback: number) {
  return rule.yearAdjust?.[year]?.threshold ?? rule.params?.threshold ?? fallback;
}

const GENERIC = /^(reading|travel(l)?ing|socialising|socializing|music|films?|movies|sports?|cooking|gym|going out|friends|netflix|gaming)$/i;

export const CHECKERS: Record<CheckerId, (ctx: CvContext, rule: CvRule) => Out> = {
  text_layer: (c) => {
    const chars = c.pageTexts.reduce((n, p) => n + p.replace(/\s/g, "").length, 0) / Math.max(1, c.pageTexts.length);
    return chars >= 200 ? ok() : bad("fail", null, "Export your CV as a text PDF from Word/Google Docs — this one looks like an image, so ATS can't read it.");
  },
  file_size: (c) => (c.fileSize <= 2.5 * 1024 * 1024 ? ok() : bad("fail", null, "Compress the PDF under 2.5 MB — larger files fail to parse in Greenhouse.")),
  page_count: (c, r) => {
    const max = r.params?.maxPages ?? 2;
    if (c.pageCount <= max) return ok();
    if (c.pageCount === max + 1 && max === 1) return bad("partial", null, "Cut to one full page — trim older or less relevant bullets.");
    return bad("fail", null, `Cut to ${max} page${max > 1 ? "s" : ""}.`);
  },
  section_headings: (c) => {
    const has = [c.cv.education.length > 0, c.cv.experience.length + c.cv.projects.length > 0, c.cv.skills.length > 0].filter(Boolean).length;
    return has === 3 ? ok() : bad(has === 2 ? "partial" : "fail", null, "Use standard headings: Education, Experience, Projects, Skills.");
  },
  education_first: (c) => {
    const i = c.cv.sectionOrder.indexOf("education");
    return i >= 0 && i <= 1 ? ok() : bad("fail", null, "Move Education to the top — students are screened on it first.");
  },
  contact_present: (c) => {
    const n = [c.cv.email, c.cv.phone].filter(Boolean).length;
    return n === 2 ? ok() : bad(n === 1 ? "partial" : "fail", null, "Add your email and UK mobile number at the top.");
  },
  uk_personal_details: (c) => {
    const m = c.text.match(/(date of birth|d\.o\.b\.?|\bdob\b|marital status|nationality|\bage:\s*\d{2})/i);
    return m ? bad("fail", m[0], "Remove date of birth, age, nationality and marital status — UK employers don't want them.") : ok();
  },
  quantified_bullets: (c, r) => {
    const bullets = allBullets(c.cv);
    if (bullets.length === 0) return na();
    const withNum = bullets.filter((b) => /\d/.test(b.text));
    const share = withNum.length / bullets.length;
    const t = thresholdFor(r, c.year, 0.4);
    if (share >= t) return ok();
    const first = bullets.find((b) => !/\d/.test(b.text))?.text ?? null;
    return bad(share >= t / 2 ? "partial" : "fail", first, "Add a real number to this bullet: how many, how much, how often, or by how much it improved.");
  },
  we_vs_i: (c) => {
    const bullets = allBullets(c.cv);
    const we = bullets.filter((b) => /\b(we|our)\b/i.test(b.text));
    if (we.length === 0) return ok();
    return bad(we.length / Math.max(1, bullets.length) > 0.2 ? "fail" : "partial", we[0].text, "Say what you did, not what the team did — start with your own action verb.");
  },
  skill_rating_glyphs: (c) => {
    const m = c.text.match(/[★☆●○■□◼◻]{2,}|\b\d{1,2}\s*\/\s*10\b/);
    return m ? bad("fail", m[0], "Replace rating bars/scores with plain labels like 'Proficient' or 'Familiar'.") : ok();
  },
  uk_grades: (c) => {
    const g = [c.cv.education.map((e) => `${e.grade ?? ""} ${e.details.map((d) => d.text).join(" ")}`).join(" ")].join(" ");
    return /(first|1st class|2:1|2\.1|2:2|upper second|predicted|\b\d{2}(\.\d)?%|a\*|\baaa\b|a-levels?|\bib\b\s*\d{2})/i.test(g)
      ? ok() : bad("fail", null, "State your (predicted) degree classification and A-level/IB grades.");
  },
  github_link: (c) =>
    /(github\.com|gitlab\.com|\.dev\b|\.io\b|portfolio)/i.test(c.text + " " + c.cv.links.join(" "))
      ? ok() : bad("fail", null, "Add your GitHub or portfolio link next to your contact details."),
  outdated_tools: (c) => {
    const m = c.text.match(/universal analytics/i);
    return m ? bad("fail", m[0], "Universal Analytics was shut down in July 2024 — say GA4 if that's what you used.") : ok();
  },
  skills_count: (c) => {
    const n = c.cv.skills.reduce((x, g) => x + g.items.length, 0);
    return n >= 5 && n <= 30 ? ok() : bad("partial", null, n < 5 ? "List the tools you actually use (aim for 8–20)." : "Trim your skills to the 8–20 most relevant.");
  },
  bullet_length: (c) => {
    const bullets = allBullets(c.cv);
    if (!bullets.length) return na();
    const long = bullets.filter((b) => b.text.split(/\s+/).length > 35);
    const share = long.length / bullets.length;
    return share <= 0.3 ? ok() : bad(share > 0.5 ? "fail" : "partial", long[0].text, "Split or tighten this bullet to one line or two.");
  },
  summary_length: (c) => (!c.cv.summary ? na() : c.cv.summary.length > 400 ? bad("partial", c.cv.summary.slice(0, 80) + "…", "Cut your profile to two lines, or remove it.") : ok()),
  interests_present: (c) => (c.cv.interests ? ok() : bad("fail", null, "Add a short, specific Interests line — recruiters read it.")),
  generic_interests: (c) => {
    if (!c.cv.interests) return na();
    const parts = c.cv.interests.split(/[,;]/).map((p) => p.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const specific = parts.filter((p) => !GENERIC.test(p) || /\d|\(/.test(p));
    return specific.length > 0 ? ok() : bad("fail", c.cv.interests, "Make interests specific: 'Half-marathon (1:52, 2025)' beats 'sports'.");
  },
  leadership_lexicon: (c) => {
    const text = [...c.cv.leadership, ...c.cv.experience].map((e) => `${e.title} ${e.bullets.map((b) => b.text).join(" ")}`).join(" ");
    return /(president|captain|founder|co-founder|head of|\bled\b|chair|treasurer|secretary|committee|officer|organis(ed|er)|organiz(ed|er)|mentor)/i.test(text)
      ? ok() : bad("fail", null, "Show a position of responsibility — a society role, captaincy, or something you organised.");
  },
  projects_count: (c) => {
    const n = c.cv.projects.length;
    return n >= 2 && n <= 4 ? ok() : bad(n === 0 ? "fail" : "partial", null, "Show 2–4 projects with what you built and the result.");
  },
  project_stack: (c) => {
    if (!c.cv.projects.length) return na();
    const withStack = c.cv.projects.filter((p) => extractSkills(`${p.title} ${p.bullets.map((b) => b.text).join(" ")}`).length > 0);
    if (withStack.length === c.cv.projects.length) return ok();
    const missing = c.cv.projects.find((p) => !withStack.includes(p));
    return bad(withStack.length ? "partial" : "fail", missing?.org ?? null, "Name the tech stack for each project (e.g. Python, FastAPI, PostgreSQL).");
  },
  spelling: (c) => {
    if (c.spellingErrors === null) return na();
    const n = c.spellingErrors.length;
    if (n === 0) return ok();
    return bad(n >= 3 ? "fail" : "partial", c.spellingErrors.slice(0, 3).join(", "), "Fix these spelling errors — even two typos measurably cut interview chances.");
  },
};

export function runCodeChecks(rules: CvRule[], ctx: CvContext): RuleResult[] {
  return rules
    .filter((r) => r.check === "code" && r.checker)
    .map((r) => (r.yearAdjust?.[ctx.year]?.skip ? { ruleId: r.id, ...na() } : { ruleId: r.id, ...CHECKERS[r.checker!](ctx, r) }));
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { stripPii, redactText } from "@/lib/cv-score/pii";
import type { ParsedCV } from "@/lib/types";

const base: ParsedCV = {
  name: "Ayse Demir", email: "ayse@example.com", phone: "+44 7700 900123", location: "London", links: ["github.com/aysedemir"], summary: null,
  education: [], experience: [], projects: [], leadership: [], skills: [], interests: null, sectionOrder: [], yearGuess: null, archetypes: [],
};

describe("PII stripping (fairness: scorer never sees identity)", () => {
  it("removes name, email, phone, location and personal links", () => {
    const s = stripPii(base);
    expect(s.name).toBe("[Candidate]");
    expect([s.email, s.phone, s.location]).toEqual([null, null, null]);
    expect(s.links).toEqual(["[link: github.com]"]);
  });
  it("two CVs differing only in identity produce identical scorer input", () => {
    const other = { ...base, name: "Mohammed Okafor", email: "mo@example.org", phone: "07123 456789", links: ["github.com/mokafor"] };
    expect(JSON.stringify(stripPii(other))).toBe(JSON.stringify(stripPii(base)));
    expect(redactText("Mohammed Okafor mo@example.org 07123 456789", other)).toBe(redactText("Ayse Demir ayse@example.com +44 7700 900123", base));
  });
});
```

`tests/cv-score/aggregate.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { aggregateCvScore } from "@/lib/cv-score/aggregate";
import type { CvRule } from "@/lib/cv-rules/types";

const r = (id: string, over: Partial<CvRule> = {}): CvRule => ({
  id, sectors: "all", dimension: "ats", statement: id, check: "code", severity: "major", evidence: "A",
  citations: [{ title: "t", publisher: "p", year: "2025", url: "https://x" }], ...over,
});
const facts = { imageOnly: false, spellingErrors: 0, hasPhone: true, hasEmail: true };
const dims = ["ats", "impact", "clarity", "relevance", "uk"] as const;
const allRules = dims.map((d) => r(`R-${d}`, { dimension: d }));

describe("aggregateCvScore", () => {
  it("all pass → 100", () => {
    const s = aggregateCvScore({ rules: allRules, results: allRules.map((x) => ({ ruleId: x.id, status: "pass", evidenceLine: null, fix: null })), sector: "consulting", year: "2nd", facts });
    expect(s.total).toBe(100);
  });
  it("a failed impact rule costs consulting more than software_eng (sector weights)", () => {
    const results = allRules.map((x) => ({ ruleId: x.id, status: x.dimension === "impact" ? "fail" as const : "pass" as const, evidenceLine: null, fix: null }));
    const con = aggregateCvScore({ rules: allRules, results, sector: "consulting", year: "2nd", facts });
    const swe = aggregateCvScore({ rules: allRules, results, sector: "software_eng", year: "2nd", facts });
    expect(con.total).toBe(65);
    expect(swe.total).toBe(75);
    expect(con.fixes[0]).toMatchObject({ ruleId: "R-impact", lostPoints: 35 });
  });
  it("na results are excluded; empty dimensions are renormalised", () => {
    const s = aggregateCvScore({ rules: [r("A")], results: [{ ruleId: "A", status: "pass", evidenceLine: null, fix: null }], sector: "consulting", year: "2nd", facts });
    expect(s.total).toBe(100);
    expect(s.dimensions.impact).toBeNull();
  });
  it("applies caps and reports them", () => {
    const pass = allRules.map((x) => ({ ruleId: x.id, status: "pass" as const, evidenceLine: null, fix: null }));
    expect(aggregateCvScore({ rules: allRules, results: pass, sector: "consulting", year: "2nd", facts: { ...facts, imageOnly: true } }))
      .toMatchObject({ total: 50, capsApplied: ["Image-only PDF: capped at 50"] });
    expect(aggregateCvScore({ rules: allRules, results: pass, sector: "consulting", year: "2nd", facts: { ...facts, hasPhone: false, hasEmail: false } }).total).toBe(40);
  });
  it("weak-evidence rules weigh less and are marked as conventions", () => {
    const rules = [r("A", { evidence: "A" }), r("D", { evidence: "D", citations: [] })];
    const s = aggregateCvScore({ rules, results: [
      { ruleId: "A", status: "pass", evidenceLine: null, fix: null }, { ruleId: "D", status: "fail", evidenceLine: null, fix: "x" }], sector: "consulting", year: "2nd", facts });
    // ats = 1.0/(1.0+0.4) → 71.4; other dims null → total 71
    expect(s.total).toBe(71);
    expect(s.fixes[0]).toMatchObject({ ruleId: "D", convention: true, citation: null });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: PII**

`lib/cv-score/pii.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV } from "@/lib/types";

export function stripPii(cv: ParsedCV): ParsedCV {
  return {
    ...cv, name: "[Candidate]", email: null, phone: null, location: null,
    links: cv.links.map((l) => `[link: ${(l.match(/(github\.com|gitlab\.com|linkedin\.com|[\w-]+\.(dev|io|com|co\.uk))/i)?.[1] ?? "website").toLowerCase()}]`),
  };
}

export function redactText(text: string, cv: ParsedCV): string {
  let t = text.replace(/[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.-]+/g, "[email]").replace(/\+?\d[\d\s()-]{8,}\d/g, "[phone]");
  for (const part of cv.name.split(/\s+/).filter((p) => p.length > 1)) t = t.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${part.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")}\\b`, "gi"), "[name]");
  return t.replace(/(\[name\]\s*)+/g, "[name] ").trim();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Aggregation**

`lib/cv-score/aggregate.ts`:

```ts
import type { Archetype, Year } from "@/lib/types";
import type { Citation, CvRule, Dimension, Evidence } from "@/lib/cv-rules/types";
import { DIMENSION_WEIGHTS } from "@/lib/cv-rules/profiles";
import type { RuleResult } from "./context";

const SEV = { critical: 3, major: 2, minor: 1 } as const;
const EVID: Record<Evidence, number> = { A: 1, B: 0.85, C: 0.6, D: 0.4 };
const CREDIT = { pass: 1, partial: 0.5, fail: 0 } as const;
const DIMS: Dimension[] = ["ats", "impact", "clarity", "relevance", "uk"];

export type Fix = { ruleId: string; statement: string; dimension: Dimension; lostPoints: number; evidenceLine: string | null; fix: string | null; citation: Citation | null; evidence: Evidence; convention: boolean };
export type CvScore = { total: number; dimensions: Record<Dimension, number | null>; capsApplied: string[]; fixes: Fix[]; results: RuleResult[] };

export function aggregateCvScore(i: {
  rules: CvRule[]; results: RuleResult[]; sector: Archetype; year: Year;
  facts: { imageOnly: boolean; spellingErrors: number; hasPhone: boolean; hasEmail: boolean };
}): CvScore {
  const byId = new Map(i.rules.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const scored = i.results
    .filter((x) => x.status !== "na" && byId.has(x.ruleId))
    .map((x) => {
      const rule = byId.get(x.ruleId)!;
      const sev = i.year && rule.yearAdjust?.[i.year]?.severity ? rule.yearAdjust[i.year]!.severity! : rule.severity;
      return { x, rule, w: SEV[sev] * EVID[rule.evidence], credit: CREDIT[x.status as keyof typeof CREDIT] };
    });

  const weights = DIMENSION_WEIGHTS[i.sector];
  const dimensions = {} as Record<Dimension, number | null>;
  const dimW: Partial<Record<Dimension, number>> = {};
  for (const d of DIMS) {
    const items = scored.filter((s) => s.rule.dimension === d);
    const den = items.reduce((n, s) => n + s.w, 0);
    dimensions[d] = den ? (100 * items.reduce((n, s) => n + s.w * s.credit, 0)) / den : null;
    dimW[d] = den;
  }
  const active = DIMS.filter((d) => dimensions[d] !== null);
  const totalWeight = active.reduce((n, d) => n + weights[d], 0);
  let total = totalWeight ? active.reduce((n, d) => n + weights[d] * dimensions[d]!, 0) / totalWeight : 0;

  const capsApplied: string[] = [];
  if (i.facts.imageOnly) { total = Math.min(total, 50); capsApplied.push("Image-only PDF: capped at 50"); }
  if (i.facts.spellingErrors >= 3) { total = Math.min(total, 50); capsApplied.push("3+ spelling errors: capped at 50"); }
  if (!i.facts.hasPhone && !i.facts.hasEmail) { total = Math.min(total, 40); capsApplied.push("No phone or email: capped at 40"); }

  const fixes: Fix[] = scored
    .filter((s) => s.credit < 1)
    .map((s) => ({
      ruleId: s.rule.id, statement: s.rule.statement, dimension: s.rule.dimension,
      lostPoints: Math.round(((weights[s.rule.dimension] / Math.max(1, totalWeight)) * 100 * s.w * (1 - s.credit)) / dimW[s.rule.dimension]!),
      evidenceLine: s.x.evidenceLine, fix: s.x.fix, citation: s.rule.citations[0] ?? null, evidence: s.rule.evidence,
      convention: s.rule.evidence === "C" || s.rule.evidence === "D",
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.lostPoints - a.lostPoints);

  for (const d of DIMS) if (dimensions[d] !== null) dimensions[d] = Math.round(dimensions[d]!);
  return { total: Math.round(total), dimensions, capsApplied, fixes, results: i.results };
}
```

Append to `lib/ai/prompts.ts`:

```ts
export const SYSTEM_JUDGE = `You are a UK graduate recruiter assessing a student CV against specific, research-backed rules.
For EACH rule id given, return one verdict: pass, partial, fail, or na (not applicable to this CV).
- evidence_line: quote the exact CV line that justifies a partial/fail (or null).
- fix: one concrete suggestion that only rephrases or reorganises what is ALREADY in the CV. Never suggest adding experience the student doesn't have. If a fix needs a number the CV lacks, write "[add your real number]" instead of inventing one.
- spelling_errors: misspelled words in the CV (British English is correct, not an error). Ignore names and technical terms.
The candidate's identity is hidden on purpose; judge only the content.`;
```

`lib/ai/judgeCvRules.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV } from "@/lib/types";
import type { CvRule } from "@/lib/cv-rules/types";
import type { RuleResult } from "@/lib/cv-score/context";
import { stripPii, redactText } from "@/lib/cv-score/pii";
import { generateStructured, runAi } from "./provider";
import { SYSTEM_JUDGE } from "./prompts";
import { JudgeOutput } from "./schemas";

export async function judgeCvRules(i: { cv: ParsedCV; text: string; rules: CvRule[]; fixtureKey?: string }) {
  const llmRules = i.rules.filter((r) => r.check === "llm");
  const ids = new Set(llmRules.map((r) => r.id));
  const out = await runAi({
    fn: "judgeCvRules",
    fixtureKey: i.fixtureKey,
    schema: JudgeOutput,
    live: () => generateStructured({
      tier: "smart", system: SYSTEM_JUDGE, schema: JudgeOutput, maxTokens: 12000,
      parts: [{ type: "text", text:
        `RULES:\n${llmRules.map((r) => `- [${r.id}] ${r.statement}${r.llmGuidance ? ` — how to judge: ${r.llmGuidance}` : ""}`).join("\n")}\n\n` +
        `CV (structured):\n${JSON.stringify(stripPii(i.cv))}\n\nCV (raw text):\n${redactText(i.text, i.cv)}` }],
    }),
    fallback: () => ({ verdicts: [], spelling_errors: null as unknown as string[] }),
  });
  const got = new Map(out.verdicts.filter((v) => ids.has(v.rule_id)).map((v) => [v.rule_id, v]));
  const results: RuleResult[] = llmRules.map((r) => {
    const v = got.get(r.id);
    return v ? { ruleId: r.id, status: v.status, evidenceLine: v.evidence_line, fix: v.fix } : { ruleId: r.id, status: "na", evidenceLine: null, fix: null };
  });
  return { results, spellingErrors: Array.isArray(out.spelling_errors) ? out.spelling_errors : null };
}
```

In fixture mode without an exact fixture, LLM rules are `na`, so the score comes from the code checks only. That is honest, and the demo persona gets a full fixture (Task 25).

- [ ] **Step 6: `scoreCv` (persisted, cached per cv × sector)**

`lib/cv-score/run.ts`:

```ts
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import type { Archetype, ParsedCV, Year } from "@/lib/types";
import { rulesFor } from "@/lib/cv-rules";
import { judgeCvRules } from "@/lib/ai/judgeCvRules";
import { runCodeChecks } from "./checkers";
import { aggregateCvScore, type CvScore } from "./aggregate";
import type { RuleResult } from "./context";
import { isImageOnly } from "@/lib/cv/pdf-text";

export async function scoreCv(supabase: SupabaseClient, i: { userId: string; cvId: string; sector: Archetype; year: Year }): Promise<CvScore> {
  const { data: cv, error } = await supabase.from("cvs").select("*").eq("id", i.cvId).single();
  if (error || !cv) throw error ?? new Error("CV not found");
  const parsed = cv.parsed_json as ParsedCV;
  const rules = rulesFor(i.sector);

  const { data: cached } = await supabase.from("cv_scores").select("rule_results").eq("cv_id", i.cvId).eq("sector", i.sector).maybeSingle();
  let llm: { results: RuleResult[]; spellingErrors: string[] | null };
  if (cached) {
    const prev = cached.rule_results as { llm: RuleResult[]; spellingErrors: string[] | null };
    llm = { results: prev.llm, spellingErrors: prev.spellingErrors };
  } else {
    // exact fixtures exist only for the fictional demo persona (Task 25)
    llm = await judgeCvRules({ cv: parsed, text: cv.raw_text, rules, fixtureKey: parsed.name === "Ayşe Demir" ? `demo-${i.sector}` : undefined });
  }
  const ctx = { text: cv.raw_text, pageTexts: cv.page_texts, pageCount: cv.page_count, fileSize: cv.file_size, cv: parsed, year: i.year, spellingErrors: llm.spellingErrors };
  const code = runCodeChecks(rules, ctx);
  const score = aggregateCvScore({
    rules, results: [...code, ...llm.results], sector: i.sector, year: i.year,
    facts: { imageOnly: isImageOnly(cv.page_texts), spellingErrors: llm.spellingErrors?.length ?? 0, hasPhone: Boolean(parsed.phone), hasEmail: Boolean(parsed.email) },
  });
  await supabase.from("cv_scores").upsert({
    cv_id: i.cvId, user_id: i.userId, sector: i.sector, total: score.total, dimensions: score.dimensions,
    caps_applied: score.capsApplied, rule_results: { code, llm: llm.results, spellingErrors: llm.spellingErrors }, fixes: score.fixes,
  }, { onConflict: "cv_id,sector" });
  return score;
}
```

`components/cv-score-panel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { ARCHETYPE_LABELS, type Archetype } from "@/lib/types";
import type { Dimension } from "@/lib/cv-rules/types";
import type { Fix } from "@/lib/cv-score/aggregate";

const DIM_LABEL: Record<Dimension, string> = { ats: "ATS readiness", impact: "Impact", clarity: "Clarity", relevance: "Relevance", uk: "UK conventions" };
const barColor = (v: number) => (v >= 75 ? "#0E8A5F" : v >= 60 ? "#5B3DF5" : "#C2410C");

export function CvScorePanel({ total, dimensions, fixes, capsApplied, sector }: {
  total: number; dimensions: Record<Dimension, number | null>; fixes: Fix[]; capsApplied: string[]; sector: Archetype;
}) {
  const dash = Math.round((total / 100) * 327);
  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-[20px] border bg-white p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-base font-bold">Your CV Score</h2>
        <span className="rounded-full bg-[#EFEBFF] px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[#3A20C4]">for {ARCHETYPE_LABELS[sector]}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <svg width="112" height="112" viewBox="0 0 120 120" role="img" aria-label={`CV score ${total} out of 100`}>
          <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="52" fill="none" stroke="#ECECF3" strokeWidth="12" />
          <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="52" fill="none" stroke="#5B3DF5" strokeWidth="12" strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray={`${dash} 327`} transform="rotate(-90 60 60)" />
          <text x="60" y="67" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="32" fontWeight="700" fill="#16161D" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-quest)" }}>{total}</text>
        </svg>
        <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1.5 text-xs">
          {(Object.keys(DIM_LABEL) as Dimension[]).filter((d) => dimensions[d] !== null).map((d) => (
            <div key={d} className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
              <div className="flex justify-between"><span>{DIM_LABEL[d]}</span><b>{dimensions[d]}</b></div>
              <div className="h-[5px] rounded-full bg-[#ECECF3]"><div className="h-[5px] rounded-full" style={{ width: `${dimensions[d]}%`, background: barColor(dimensions[d]!) }} /></div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      {capsApplied.map((c) => <p key={c} className="rounded-xl bg-[#FFF1DC] px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#7A4100]">{c}</p>)}
      <ol className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t pt-3">
        {fixes.slice(0, 5).map((f, i) => (
          <li key={f.ruleId} className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">{i === 0 ? "Quick win" : DIM_LABEL[f.dimension]}{f.convention ? " · convention" : ""}</span>
              <span className="chip-xp">+{f.lostPoints} pts</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm leading-snug">{f.fix ?? f.statement}</p>
            {f.evidenceLine && <p className="text-xs italic text-muted-foreground">“{f.evidenceLine}”</p>}
            {f.citation
              ? <a href={f.citation.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="self-start rounded-lg bg-[#F2F2F7] px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[#3F3F4C] no-underline">Source: {f.citation.publisher} · Evidence {f.evidence}</a>
              : <span className="self-start rounded-lg bg-[#F2F2F7] px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[#3F3F4C]">Common practice · Evidence {f.evidence}</span>}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { ARCHETYPES, type Archetype, type ArchetypeScore } from "@/lib/types";

type Opt = { id: string; label: string; weights: Partial<Record<Archetype, number>> };
export const QUESTIONS: { id: string; prompt: string; options: Opt[] }[] = [
  { id: "fun", prompt: "Which sounds most fun?", options: [
    { id: "build", label: "Building an app people use", weights: { software_eng: 3, product: 1 } },
    { id: "patterns", label: "Finding patterns in messy data", weights: { data_analytics: 3, research: 1 } },
    { id: "puzzles", label: "Solving puzzles against the clock", weights: { quant_trading: 3, software_eng: 1 } },
    { id: "business", label: "Cracking a company's big problem", weights: { consulting: 3, finance_banking: 1 } },
    { id: "markets", label: "Following the markets", weights: { finance_banking: 3, quant_trading: 1 } },
    { id: "audience", label: "Growing an audience", weights: { marketing_growth: 3, product: 1 } },
  ] },
  { id: "tuesday", prompt: "Your ideal Tuesday at work", options: [
    { id: "flow", label: "Deep in code, headphones on", weights: { software_eng: 3 } },
    { id: "dash", label: "Spreadsheets turning into a dashboard", weights: { data_analytics: 2, finance_banking: 2 } },
    { id: "floor", label: "A fast-paced trading floor", weights: { quant_trading: 2, finance_banking: 2 } },
    { id: "client", label: "Running a client workshop", weights: { consulting: 3 } },
    { id: "users", label: "Talking to users about what they need", weights: { product: 2, marketing_growth: 2 } },
    { id: "lab", label: "Reading papers and running experiments", weights: { research: 3 } },
  ] },
  { id: "power", prompt: "Pick a superpower", options: [
    { id: "logic", label: "Flawless logic", weights: { software_eng: 2, quant_trading: 2 } },
    { id: "story", label: "Storytelling", weights: { marketing_growth: 2, consulting: 2 } },
    { id: "numbers", label: "Instant maths", weights: { data_analytics: 2, finance_banking: 2, quant_trading: 1 } },
    { id: "empathy", label: "Reading people", weights: { product: 2, marketing_growth: 1 } },
    { id: "curiosity", label: "Endless curiosity", weights: { research: 2, data_analytics: 2 } },
    { id: "persuasion", label: "Persuasion", weights: { consulting: 2, finance_banking: 2 } },
  ] },
  { id: "group", prompt: "In a group project you're usually the…", options: [
    { id: "builder", label: "Builder", weights: { software_eng: 3 } },
    { id: "analyst", label: "Analyst", weights: { data_analytics: 3 } },
    { id: "planner", label: "Planner", weights: { product: 2, consulting: 2 } },
    { id: "presenter", label: "Presenter", weights: { consulting: 2, marketing_growth: 2 } },
    { id: "researcher", label: "Researcher", weights: { research: 3 } },
    { id: "treasurer", label: "Money person", weights: { finance_banking: 3 } },
  ] },
  { id: "module", prompt: "Which module would you pick?", options: [
    { id: "algo", label: "Algorithms", weights: { software_eng: 2, quant_trading: 2 } },
    { id: "stats", label: "Statistics", weights: { data_analytics: 2, research: 2 } },
    { id: "corpfin", label: "Corporate finance", weights: { finance_banking: 3 } },
    { id: "mkt", label: "Marketing strategy", weights: { marketing_growth: 3 } },
    { id: "game", label: "Game theory", weights: { quant_trading: 2, consulting: 2 } },
    { id: "design", label: "Design thinking", weights: { product: 3 } },
  ] },
  { id: "matters", prompt: "What matters most in your first internship?", options: [
    { id: "ship", label: "Shipping real code", weights: { software_eng: 3 } },
    { id: "tools", label: "Learning tools like SQL and Python", weights: { data_analytics: 3 } },
    { id: "prestige", label: "Prestige and pay", weights: { finance_banking: 2, quant_trading: 2 } },
    { id: "variety", label: "A variety of problems", weights: { consulting: 3 } },
    { id: "own", label: "Owning a feature end to end", weights: { product: 3 } },
    { id: "creative", label: "Creative freedom", weights: { marketing_growth: 3 } },
    { id: "depth", label: "Going deep on one topic", weights: { research: 3 } },
  ] },
];

export function scoreQuiz(answers: Record<string, string>): ArchetypeScore[] {
  const totals = Object.fromEntries(ARCHETYPES.map((a) => [a, 0])) as Record<Archetype, number>;
  const why = Object.fromEntries(ARCHETYPES.map((a) => [a, [] as string[]])) as Record<Archetype, string[]>;
  for (const q of QUESTIONS) {
    const o = q.options.find((x) => x.id === answers[q.id]);
    if (!o) continue;
    for (const [k, w] of Object.entries(o.weights) as [Archetype, number][]) {
      totals[k] += w;
      if (w >= 2) why[k].push(`You picked “${o.label}”`);
    }
  }
  const max = Math.max(1, ...Object.values(totals));
  return ARCHETYPES.map((key) => ({ key, score: Math.round((totals[key] / max) * 100), why: why[key].slice(0, 3) }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, 3);
}
```

```ts
export const SYSTEM_QUIZ = `A UK student with no CV answered a 6-question interests quiz. Using the pre-computed ranking given, return the same top 3 archetypes (you may adjust scores by at most 10 points), each with 2 short, warm "why" lines that reference their answers, plus 5 beginner-friendly skills worth learning for the top archetype.`;
```

`lib/ai/quizToArchetypes.ts`:

```ts
import { QUESTIONS, scoreQuiz } from "@/lib/quiz/questions";
import { canonicalize } from "@/lib/skills/extract";
import { SKILLS } from "@/lib/skills/vocab";
import { generateStructured, runAi } from "./provider";
import { SYSTEM_QUIZ } from "./prompts";
import { QuizOutput } from "./schemas";

export async function quizToArchetypes(answers: Record<string, string>) {
  const ranking = scoreQuiz(answers);
  const out = await runAi({
    fn: "quizToArchetypes", schema: QuizOutput,
    live: () => generateStructured({
      tier: "fast", system: SYSTEM_QUIZ, schema: QuizOutput, maxTokens: 2000,
      parts: [{ type: "text", text: `Answers:\n${QUESTIONS.map((q) => `${q.prompt} → ${q.options.find((o) => o.id === answers[q.id])?.label ?? "-"}`).join("\n")}\n\nRanking: ${JSON.stringify(ranking)}` }],
    }),
    fallback: () => ({ archetypes: ranking, skills: SKILLS.filter((s) => s.archetypes.includes(ranking[0].key)).slice(0, 5).map((s) => s.canonical) }),
  });
  const keys = new Set(ranking.map((r) => r.key));
  return {
    archetypes: out.archetypes.filter((a) => keys.has(a.key)).length === 3 ? out.archetypes : ranking,
    skills: out.skills.map((s) => canonicalize(s) ?? s),
  };
}
```

`app/(app)/profile/quiz/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { awardXP } from "@/lib/gamify/award";
import { quizToArchetypes } from "@/lib/ai/quizToArchetypes";
import { QUESTIONS } from "@/lib/quiz/questions";

export async function submitQuiz(answers: Record<string, string>) {
  if (QUESTIONS.some((q) => !answers[q.id])) return { error: "Answer all six questions." };
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const r = await quizToArchetypes(answers);
  const { data: p } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("target_archetype").eq("user_id", user.id).single();
  await supabase.from("profiles").update({
    archetypes: r.archetypes, target_archetype: p?.target_archetype ?? r.archetypes[0].key,
  }).eq("user_id", user.id);
  const award = await awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: "quiz_done", profileComplete: true });
  return { award };
}
```

`app/(app)/profile/quiz/page.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Progress } from "@/components/ui/progress";
import { QUESTIONS } from "@/lib/quiz/questions";
import { celebrate } from "@/components/reward-toast";
import { submitQuiz } from "./actions";

export default function Quiz() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [i, setI] = useState(0);
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const q = QUESTIONS[i];
  function pick(id: string) {
    const next = { ...answers, [q.id]: id };
    setAnswers(next);
    if (i < QUESTIONS.length - 1) { setI(i + 1); return; }
    start(async () => {
      const r = await submitQuiz(next);
      if ("award" in r) celebrate(r.award);
      router.push("/profile");
    });
  }
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-2xl flex-col gap-6">
      <Progress value={((i + 1) / QUESTIONS.length) * 100} />
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold">{q.prompt}</h1>
      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
        {q.options.map((o) => (
          <button key={o.id} type="button" disabled={pending} onClick={() => pick(o.id)} className="card-quest min-h-16 px-4 py-3 text-left font-bold hover:border-primary">
            {o.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {pending && <p role="status" className="font-semibold text-[#3A20C4]">Finding your roles…</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```
