```ts
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { scoreCv } from "@/lib/cv-score/run";
import { ARCHETYPES, type Archetype } from "@/lib/types";

export async function setTargetSector(sector: Archetype) {
  if (!ARCHETYPES.includes(sector)) return;
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  await supabase.from("profiles").update({ target_archetype: sector }).eq("user_id", user.id);
  const [{ data: cv }, { data: p }] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from("cvs").select("id").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("is_active", true).maybeSingle(),
    supabase.from("profiles").select("year").eq("user_id", user.id).single(),
  ]);
  if (cv) await scoreCv(supabase, { userId: user.id, cvId: cv.id, sector, year: p?.year ?? "2nd" });
  revalidatePath("/profile");
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { getProfile } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import { ARCHETYPE_LABELS, type Archetype } from "@/lib/types";
import { CvScorePanel } from "@/components/cv-score-panel";
import { MythCard } from "@/components/myth-card";
import { SectorSelect } from "./sector-select";

export default async function ProfilePage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<{ warn?: string }> }) {
  const { warn } = await searchParams;
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const p = await getProfile(supabase, user.id);
  const sector = (p.target_archetype ?? p.archetypes[0]?.key ?? "consulting") as Archetype;
  const { data: cv } = await supabase.from("cvs").select("id").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("is_active", true).maybeSingle();
  const { data: score } = cv
    ? await supabase.from("cv_scores").select("*").eq("cv_id", cv.id).eq("sector", sector).maybeSingle()
    : { data: null };
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[340px_1fr]">
      <aside className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {score ? <CvScorePanel total={score.total} dimensions={score.dimensions} fixes={score.fixes} capsApplied={score.caps_applied} sector={sector} />
          : <div className="card-quest p-5"><p className="font-bold">No CV score yet</p><Link href="/profile/build" className="btn-quest mt-3 block py-3 text-center">Upload your CV</Link></div>}
        <MythCard />
      </aside>
      <section className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {warn === "image" && <p className="rounded-xl bg-[#FFF1DC] p-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[#7A4100]">Your PDF looks like an image, so ATS systems can&apos;t read it. Export it as a text PDF — or take the quiz to keep going.</p>}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
          <div><h1 className="text-[28px] font-extrabold tracking-tight">Roles that fit you</h1><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Based on your {cv ? "CV" : "quiz answers"}.</p></div>
          {cv && <SectorSelect value={sector} />}
        </div>
        {p.archetypes.slice(0, 3).map((a) => (
          <article key={a.key} className="flex items-start gap-4 rounded-[18px] border bg-white p-5">
            <div className="num-quest flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[14px] bg-[#EFEBFF] text-lg text-[#3A20C4]">{a.score}%</div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{ARCHETYPE_LABELS[a.key]} internships</h2>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{a.why.map((w) => <li key={w}>{w}</li>)}</ul>
            </div>
          </article>
        ))}
        <Link href="/feed" className="btn-quest self-start px-6 py-3">See my internships →</Link>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```sql
create table public.playbook_progress (
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users on delete cascade,
  action_id text not null,
  status text not null check (status in ('planned','done')),
  evidence_note text,
  evidence_url text,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (user_id, action_id),
  constraint done_needs_evidence check (status <> 'done' or length(trim(coalesce(evidence_note,''))) >= 10)
);
alter table public.playbook_progress enable row level security;
create policy "own rows" on public.playbook_progress for all using (auth.uid() = user_id) with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Schema and build script**

`lib/playbooks/schema.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { YEARS } from "@/lib/types";

export const Source = z.object({
  title: z.string().optional(), publisher: z.string().optional(), url: z.string().url().optional(),
  primary: z.boolean().optional(), quote: z.string().min(1), accessed: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/),
});
export const Signal = z.object({
  id: z.string(), text: z.string(), grade: z.enum(["A", "B", "C", "D"]), basis: z.string().optional(),
  note: z.string().optional(), ruleIds: z.array(z.string()), sources: z.array(Source).min(1),
});
export const Action = z.object({
  id: z.string(), type: z.enum(["project", "course", "experience"]), title: z.string(), description: z.string(),
  skills: z.array(z.string()), signals: z.array(z.string()), effort: z.enum(["weekend", "2-4 weeks", "term"]),
  minYear: z.enum(YEARS), evidence: z.string(), url: z.string().url().optional(), howToFind: z.string().optional(),
  verified: z.boolean().nullable().optional(), expiresOn: z.string().optional(), reviewBy: z.string().optional(),
  sources: z.array(Source),
});
export const Playbook = z.object({
  signals: z.array(Signal), actions: z.array(Action),
  yearGuidance: z.record(z.enum(YEARS), z.string()),
  yearGuidanceDated: z.array(z.object({ year: z.enum(YEARS), text: z.string(), expiresOn: z.string().optional(), ref: z.string().optional() })).default([]),
  note: z.string().optional(),
});
export type Signal = z.infer<typeof Signal>;
export type Action = z.infer<typeof Action>;
export type Playbook = z.infer<typeof Playbook>;
```

`scripts/build-playbooks.ts`:
- Reads both research JSONs.
- Inlines tech `sources` refs (`{ref, quote, accessed}` + lookup in the top-level `sources[]` by `id`).
- Maps `minYear` (`1→"1st"`, `2→"2nd"`, strings unchanged).
- Maps the finance year keys (`year1→1st`, `year2_penultimate→2nd` and `penultimate`, `final→final`, `postgrad→masters`, `note→note`).
- Maps finance `yearGuidanceDated[].year` the same way.
- Drops research-only fields (`hours`, `free`, `cost`, `signalValue`, `timing`, `kind`, `check`), except that `timing` is appended to `description` when present.
- Parses each archetype with `Playbook`, then runs cross-checks and **throws** (non-zero exit) on:
  - a missing archetype;
  - a duplicate id;
  - a dangling signal ref;
  - a non-canonical skill;
  - an unknown `ruleId`, checked against `RULES_BY_ID` from `lib/cv-rules`. Sub-item ids such as `SWE-17a` or `DAT-11c` resolve to their base id (`/^([A-Z]+-\d+)[a-z]?$/`), and an id is unknown only if the base id is missing too;
  - an action that has a `url` without `verified === true`.
- Writes `lib/playbooks/data.json` (pretty, stable key order).
- Exports `normalise(raw, techSources)` for the test.

`lib/playbooks/index.ts`:

```ts
import data from "./data.json";
import { Playbook } from "./schema";
import { ARCHETYPES, type Archetype } from "@/lib/types";
export const PLAYBOOKS = Object.fromEntries(ARCHETYPES.map((a) => [a, Playbook.parse((data as Record<string, unknown>)[a])])) as Record<Archetype, Playbook>;
export type { Playbook, Action, Signal } from "./schema";
```

Add `"build-playbooks": "tsx scripts/build-playbooks.ts"` to `package.json`, run it, then run the tests → PASS. Fix the data, not the checks: if a check fails, report the item. Never relax a check.

- [ ] **Step 5: `lib/target/market.ts`**

```ts
import { computeCoverage, type CoverageResult, type CvSkillSource } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { isOpen } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";
import { ARCHETYPE_LABELS, type Archetype } from "@/lib/types";

export type DemandJob = { id: string; archetype: string | null; is_live: boolean; deadline: string | null; requirements: { skill: string | null; section: "must" | "nice" }[] };
export type DemandItem = { skill: string; jobs: number; share: number; mustShare: number; section: "must" | "nice" };
export type MarketDemand = { archetype: Archetype; n: number; smallSample: boolean; items: DemandItem[] };

export const SMALL_SAMPLE = 5;
const MAX_ITEMS = 12;

export function marketDemand(jobs: DemandJob[], archetype: Archetype, today: string): MarketDemand {
  const pool = jobs.filter((j) => j.archetype === archetype && j.is_live && isOpen(j.deadline, today));
  const seen = new Map<string, { jobs: number; must: number }>();
  for (const j of pool) {
    const per = new Map<string, boolean>(); // skill -> is must in this job
    for (const r of j.requirements) if (r.skill) per.set(r.skill, (per.get(r.skill) ?? false) || r.section === "must");
    for (const [skill, must] of per) {
      const e = seen.get(skill) ?? { jobs: 0, must: 0 };
      e.jobs += 1; if (must) e.must += 1; seen.set(skill, e);
    }
  }
  const n = pool.length;
  const items = [...seen.entries()]
    .map(([skill, e]) => ({ skill, jobs: e.jobs, share: e.jobs / Math.max(1, n), mustShare: e.must / e.jobs, section: (e.must / e.jobs >= 0.5 ? "must" : "nice") as "must" | "nice" }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.jobs - a.jobs || a.skill.localeCompare(b.skill))
    .slice(0, MAX_ITEMS);
  return { archetype, n, smallSample: n < SMALL_SAMPLE, items };
}

export function readiness(d: MarketDemand, cv: CvSkillSource): { coverage: CoverageResult; pct: number | null; label: string } {
  const coverage = computeCoverage(d.items.map(({ skill, section }) => ({ skill, section })), cv);
  const role = ARCHETYPE_LABELS[d.archetype];
  if (d.smallSample || coverage.weighted === null) return { coverage, pct: null, label: `Small sample — ${d.n} live ${role} roles` };
  let pct = Math.round(coverage.weighted * 100);
  if (pct >= 100 && coverage.items.some((i) => i.status !== "existing")) pct = 99;
  return { coverage, pct, label: `Readiness vs ${d.n} live ${role} internships` };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Failing tests for action picking**

`tests/target/actions.test.ts` should cover:
- `minYear` above the student's year is excluded (`penultimate` action hidden from a `1st`-year student).
- `expiresOn < today` is hidden; `reviewBy < today` gives `stale: true`.
- Done actions are excluded.
- Ordering: more gap skills covered first, then `weekend < 2-4 weeks < term`, then `project > experience > course`.
- At most 2 actions per gap and 6 in total.
- With no gap matches: `fallback: true` and 3 actions.
- `visibleHints` returns one hint per `supportedByResume` item and none for `existing`/`gap`.

Year order is the index in `YEARS`; `masters` ranks above `final`.

- [ ] **Step 7: `lib/target/actions.ts`**

```ts
import type { Action, Playbook } from "@/lib/playbooks";
import type { CoverageResult } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { YEARS, type Year } from "@/lib/types";

const EFFORT = { weekend: 0, "2-4 weeks": 1, term: 2 } as const;
const TYPE = { project: 0, experience: 1, course: 2 } as const;
export type PickedAction = Action & { covers: string[]; stale: boolean };

export function isActive(x: { expiresOn?: string; reviewBy?: string }, today: string) {
  return { show: !x.expiresOn || x.expiresOn >= today, stale: !!x.reviewBy && x.reviewBy < today };
}

export function pickActions(pb: Playbook, gaps: string[], year: Year, today: string, done: Set<string>) {
  const yi = YEARS.indexOf(year);
  const pool = pb.actions
    .filter((a) => YEARS.indexOf(a.minYear) <= yi && !done.has(a.id) && isActive(a, today).show)
    .map((a) => ({ ...a, covers: a.skills.filter((s) => gaps.includes(s)), stale: isActive(a, today).stale }));
  const rank = (a: PickedAction, b: PickedAction) =>
    b.covers.length - a.covers.length || EFFORT[a.effort] - EFFORT[b.effort] || TYPE[a.type] - TYPE[b.type] || a.id.localeCompare(b.id);
  const matched = pool.filter((a) => a.covers.length > 0).sort(rank);
  if (matched.length === 0) return { actions: pool.filter((a) => a.signals.length > 0).sort(rank).slice(0, 3), fallback: true };
  const perGap = new Map<string, number>(); const out: PickedAction[] = [];
  for (const a of matched) {
    if (out.length === 6) break;
    if (a.covers.every((g) => (perGap.get(g) ?? 0) >= 2)) continue;
    out.push(a); for (const g of a.covers) perGap.set(g, (perGap.get(g) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  return { actions: out, fallback: false };
}

export function visibleHints(c: CoverageResult): string[] {
  return c.items.filter((i) => i.status === "supportedByResume")
    .map((i) => `Your CV mentions ${i.skill} but not in your skills list or bullets — add a bullet about what you did with it.`);
}
```

Run → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 9: Server actions**

`app/(app)/profile/target/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { awardXP } from "@/lib/gamify/award";
import { PLAYBOOKS } from "@/lib/playbooks";
import { ARCHETYPES } from "@/lib/types";

function findAction(id: string) {
  for (const a of ARCHETYPES) { const x = PLAYBOOKS[a].actions.find((y) => y.id === id); if (x) return x; }
  return null;
}

export async function planAction(id: string) {
  if (!findAction(id)) return { error: "Unknown action." };
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  await supabase.from("playbook_progress").upsert({ user_id: user.id, action_id: id, status: "planned", updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
  revalidatePath("/profile/target");
  return { ok: true };
}

export async function completeAction(id: string, note: string, url?: string) {
  const action = findAction(id);
  const clean = note.trim();
  if (!action) return { error: "Unknown action." };
  if (clean.length < 10) return { error: "Tell us in a sentence what you did — this is what lets us use it on your CV." };
  if (url && !/^https:\/\//.test(url)) return { error: "Links must start with https://" };
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const { data: prev } = await supabase.from("playbook_progress").select("status").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("action_id", id).maybeSingle();
  await supabase.from("playbook_progress").upsert({ user_id: user.id, action_id: id, status: "done", evidence_note: clean, evidence_url: url ?? null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
  // The student's own words become a confirmed fact — the only way a playbook item reaches tailoring.
  await supabase.from("confirmed_facts").insert({ user_id: user.id, claim: `${action.title}: ${clean}${url ? ` (${url})` : ""}`, source: "user-stated" });
  const award = prev?.status === "done" ? null : await awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: "playbook_done" });
  revalidatePath("/profile/target");
  return { award };
}
```

The XP is idempotent: re-completing an action gives no XP. The note is stored verbatim; nothing is generated.

- [ ] **Step 2: Queries**

Append to `lib/db/queries.ts`:

```ts
import type { JobForScoring, ParsedCV } from "@/lib/types";

export type JobListItem = JobForScoring & { company: string; url: string; summary: string; extId: string };

type JobDbRow = {
  id: string; ext_id: string; title: string; url: string; location: string; remote: boolean; deadline: string | null; posted_at: string | null;
  archetype: JobForScoring["archetype"]; year_eligibility: JobForScoring["yearEligibility"]; sponsors_visa: JobForScoring["sponsorsVisa"];
  employment_type: JobForScoring["employmentType"]; first_year_friendly: boolean; requirements: JobForScoring["requirements"];
  text_lines: string[]; summary: string; companies: { name: string } | null;
};

const JOB_COLUMNS = "id, ext_id, title, url, location, remote, deadline, posted_at, archetype, year_eligibility, sponsors_visa, employment_type, first_year_friendly, requirements, text_lines, summary, companies(name)";

function toJob(r: JobDbRow): JobListItem {
  return {
    id: r.id, extId: r.ext_id, title: r.title, url: r.url, location: r.location, remote: r.remote, deadline: r.deadline, postedAt: r.posted_at,
    archetype: r.archetype, yearEligibility: r.year_eligibility, sponsorsVisa: r.sponsors_visa, employmentType: r.employment_type,
    firstYearFriendly: r.first_year_friendly, requirements: r.requirements ?? [], textLines: r.text_lines ?? [], summary: r.summary,
    company: r.companies?.name ?? "",
  };
}

export async function getLiveJobs(supabase: SupabaseClient, today: string): Promise<JobListItem[]> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("jobs").select(JOB_COLUMNS).eq("is_live", true)
    .or(`deadline.is.null,deadline.gte.${today}`).limit(500);
  if (error) throw error;
  return ((data ?? []) as unknown as JobDbRow[]).map(toJob);
}

export async function getJob(supabase: SupabaseClient, id: string): Promise<JobListItem | null> {
  const { data } = await supabase.from("jobs").select(JOB_COLUMNS).eq("id", id).maybeSingle();
  return data ? toJob(data as unknown as JobDbRow) : null;
}

export async function getActiveCv(supabase: SupabaseClient, userId: string) {
  const { data } = await supabase.from("cvs").select("id, parsed_json, raw_text").eq("user_id", userId).eq("is_active", true).maybeSingle();
  return data ? { id: data.id as string, parsed: data.parsed_json as ParsedCV, rawText: data.raw_text as string } : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Ranking**

`lib/feed/rank.ts`:

```ts
import type { FitLabel, StudentProfile } from "@/lib/types";
import type { JobListItem } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import { computeCoverage, jdRequirementSkills, type CoverageResult, type CvSkillSource } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { scoreFit, type FitResult } from "@/lib/scoring/fit";
import { isOpen } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";

export type Ranked = { job: JobListItem; fit: FitResult; coverage: CoverageResult };

export function rankJobs(student: StudentProfile, cv: CvSkillSource, jobs: JobListItem[], today: string): Ranked[] {
  return jobs
    .filter((j) => isOpen(j.deadline, today))
    .map((job) => {
      const coverage = computeCoverage(jdRequirementSkills(job.textLines, job.requirements), cv);
      return { job, coverage, fit: scoreFit(student, job, coverage, today) };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => Number(a.fit.capped) - Number(b.fit.capped) || b.fit.global - a.fit.global);
}

const APPLY: Record<FitLabel, number> = { "Strong fit": 50, "Good fit": 50, Stretch: 25, "Not recommended": 10 };
export const applyXp = (l: FitLabel) => APPLY[l];
```

Run `npx vitest run tests/feed` → PASS.

```ts
"use server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { awardXP } from "@/lib/gamify/award";

export async function saveJob(jobId: string) {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const { error } = await supabase.from("applications").upsert(
    { user_id: user.id, job_id: jobId, status: "saved" },
    { onConflict: "user_id,job_id", ignoreDuplicates: true },
  );
  if (error) return null;
  return awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: "job_saved" }, jobId);
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { celebrate } from "./reward-toast";
import { saveJob } from "@/app/(app)/jobs/actions";

export function SaveButton({ jobId, initiallySaved }: { jobId: string; initiallySaved: boolean }) {
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(initiallySaved);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  return (
    <button type="button" aria-pressed={saved} aria-label={saved ? "Saved" : "Save job"} disabled={pending || saved}
      onClick={() => start(async () => { celebrate(await saveJob(jobId)); setSaved(true); })}
      className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border hover:bg-[#EFEBFF]">
      <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill={saved ? "#5B3DF5" : "none"} stroke="#5B3DF5" strokeWidth="2" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 3h12v18l-6-4-6 4z" /></svg>
    </button>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import type { Ranked } from "@/lib/feed/rank";
import { coverageLabel } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { deadlineLabel } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";
import { applyXp } from "@/lib/feed/rank";
import { FitBadge } from "./fit-badge";
import { SaveButton } from "./save-button";

export function JobCard({ r, today, saved }: { r: Ranked; today: string; saved: boolean }) {
  const gate = r.fit.gates.find((g) => !g.passed);
  const sub = gate?.reason ?? (r.coverage.readable ? `${r.coverage.must.matched}/${r.coverage.must.total} must-haves` : coverageLabel(r.coverage));
  return (
    <article className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-[18px] border bg-white px-5 py-4">
      <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[14px] bg-[#EFEBFF] text-lg font-extrabold text-[#3A20C4]">{r.job.company.slice(0, 1)}</div>
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1">
        <Link href={`/jobs/${r.job.id}`} className="text-base font-bold text-foreground no-underline hover:underline">{r.job.title}</Link>
        <div className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">{r.job.company} · {r.job.location || "Location not stated"} · {sub}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="whitespace-nowrap text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#8A4B00]">{deadlineLabel(r.job.deadline, today)}</div>
      <FitBadge label={r.fit.label} score={r.fit.global} />
      <span className="chip-xp">+{applyXp(r.fit.label)} XP</span>
      <SaveButton jobId={r.job.id} initiallySaved={saved} />
      <Link href={`/jobs/${r.job.id}`} className="btn-quest px-4 py-2.5 no-underline">View</Link>
    </article>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Feed page**

`app/(app)/feed/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { getActiveCv, getLiveJobs, getProfile, toStudent } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import { rankJobs } from "@/lib/feed/rank";
import { cvSkillSource, profileSkillSource } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { daysUntil, todayLondon } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";
import { ARCHETYPE_LABELS, type Archetype } from "@/lib/types";
import { JobCard } from "@/components/job-card";

export default async function Feed({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<{ a?: string; soon?: string }> }) {
  const { a, soon } = await searchParams;
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const today = todayLondon();
  const [profile, cv, jobs, { data: apps }] = await Promise.all([
    getProfile(supabase, user.id), getActiveCv(supabase, user.id), getLiveJobs(supabase, today),
    supabase.from("applications").select("job_id").eq("user_id", user.id),
  ]);
  const student = toStudent(profile);
  let ranked = rankJobs(student, cv ? cvSkillSource(cv.parsed) : profileSkillSource(student.skills), jobs, today);
  if (a) ranked = ranked.filter((r) => r.job.archetype === a);
  if (soon) ranked = ranked.filter((r) => r.job.deadline && daysUntil(r.job.deadline, today) <= 7);
  const savedIds = new Set((apps ?? []).map((x) => x.job_id));
  const chips: [string, string | undefined][] = [["All", undefined], ...student.archetypes.slice(0, 3).map((x) => [ARCHETYPE_LABELS[x.key], x.key] as [string, Archetype])];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3.5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[28px] font-extrabold tracking-tight">Internships for you</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{ranked.length} live roles, ranked by how well they fit you.</p>
        </div>
        <nav className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" aria-label="Filters">
          {chips.map(([label, key]) => (
            <Link key={label} href={key ? `/feed?a=${key}` : "/feed"}
              className={`rounded-full border px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold no-underline ${a === key ? "border-foreground bg-foreground text-white" : "bg-white text-foreground"}`}>{label}</Link>
          ))}
          <Link href={soon ? "/feed" : `/feed?soon=1${a ? `&a=${a}` : ""}`}
            className={`rounded-full border px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold no-underline ${soon ? "border-foreground bg-foreground text-white" : "bg-white text-foreground"}`}>Closing soon</Link>
        </nav>
      </div>
      {ranked.length === 0 && <p className="card-quest p-6 text-center font-semibold">No live roles match these filters right now — try “All”.</p>}
      {ranked.slice(0, 60).map((r) => <JobCard key={r.job.id} r={r} today={today} saved={savedIds.has(r.job.id)} />)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement ordering**

`lib/scoring/requirements.ts`:

```ts
import type { Importance, Requirement } from "@/lib/types";

export type RequirementRow = { text: string; skill: string | null; importance: Importance; evidenceTier: Requirement["evidenceTier"]; match: "strong" | "partial" | "missing"; cvEvidence: string | null };

const RANK: Record<Importance, number> = { critical: 0, high: 1, meaningful: 2, preferred: 3, low_signal: 4 };
const MATCH_RANK = { missing: 0, partial: 1, strong: 2 } as const;

export function orderRequirementRows(rows: RequirementRow[]): RequirementRow[] {
  const capped = rows.map((r) => (r.evidenceTier === "inferred" && RANK[r.importance] < RANK.meaningful ? { ...r, importance: "meaningful" as const } : r));
  const sorted = [...capped].sort((a, b) => RANK[a.importance] - RANK[b.importance] || MATCH_RANK[a.match] - MATCH_RANK[b.match]);
  const keep = sorted.filter((r) => RANK[r.importance] <= RANK.high);
  const rest = sorted.filter((r) => RANK[r.importance] > RANK.high);
  return [...keep, ...rest.slice(0, Math.max(0, 12 - keep.length))];
}
```

```ts
export const SYSTEM_EXPLAIN = `You explain to a UK student how well their CV fits one internship.
${UNTRUSTED_JD_RULE}
You receive a numbered list of the posting's requirements whose importance was ALREADY fixed from the posting alone — do not re-rate importance.
For each requirement index: match = strong (quote the exact CV line in cv_evidence), partial, or missing.
why_fit: 2-3 short, specific, encouraging lines grounded in the CV.
gaps: for each partial/missing requirement, say if it blocks (critical/high only), name adjacent experience they DO have, and one concrete way to close it (a small project, a free course, how to phrase existing experience). Never suggest claiming experience they don't have.`;
```

Append to `lib/ai/heuristics.ts`:

```ts
import type { CoverageResult } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import type { RequirementRow } from "@/lib/scoring/requirements";

export function heuristicExplain(i: { requirements: Requirement[]; coverage: CoverageResult; cvText: string; archetypeMatch: boolean }) {
  const status = new Map(i.coverage.items.map((x) => [x.skill.toLowerCase(), x.status]));
  const lines = i.cvText.split("\n");
  const rows: RequirementRow[] = i.requirements.map((r) => {
    const s = r.skill ? status.get(r.skill.toLowerCase()) : undefined;
    const match = s === "existing" ? "strong" : s === "supportedByResume" ? "partial" : s === "gap" ? "missing" : "partial";
    return { text: r.text, skill: r.skill, importance: r.importance, evidenceTier: r.evidenceTier, match,
      cvEvidence: r.skill && match !== "missing" ? lines.find((l) => l.toLowerCase().includes(r.skill!.toLowerCase())) ?? null : null };
  });
  const matched = rows.filter((r) => r.match === "strong" && r.skill).map((r) => r.skill);
  const whyFit = [
    ...(i.archetypeMatch ? ["This role matches the kind of work your profile points to."] : []),
    ...(matched.length ? [`Your CV already shows ${matched.slice(0, 3).join(", ")}.`] : []),
  ];
  const gaps = rows.filter((r) => r.match !== "strong").map((r) => ({
    requirement: r.text, blocking: r.importance === "critical" || r.importance === "high", adjacentExperience: null,
    howToClose: r.skill ? `Do a small project with ${r.skill} (a weekend is enough) and add it to Projects.` : "Look for a society role or module that shows this, and describe it on your CV.",
  }));
  return { rows, whyFit, gaps };
}
```

`lib/ai/explainMatch.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, Requirement, StudentProfile } from "@/lib/types";
import type { JobListItem } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import type { CoverageResult } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { orderRequirementRows, type RequirementRow } from "@/lib/scoring/requirements";
import { stripPii } from "@/lib/cv-score/pii";
import { generateStructured, runAi } from "./provider";
import { SYSTEM_EXPLAIN, wrapJob } from "./prompts";
import { ExplainOutput } from "./schemas";
import { heuristicExplain } from "./heuristics";

export type Explanation = { rows: RequirementRow[]; whyFit: string[]; gaps: { requirement: string; blocking: boolean; adjacentExperience: string | null; howToClose: string }[] };

function requirementsFor(job: JobListItem, coverage: CoverageResult): Requirement[] {
  if (job.requirements.length) return job.requirements;
  return coverage.items.map((c) => ({ text: c.skill, skill: c.skill, importance: c.section === "must" ? "high" : "preferred", section: c.section, evidenceTier: "structural" }));
}

export async function explainMatch(i: { cv: ParsedCV | null; cvText: string; student: StudentProfile; job: JobListItem; coverage: CoverageResult }): Promise<Explanation> {
  const reqs = requirementsFor(i.job, i.coverage);
  const fallback = () => heuristicExplain({ requirements: reqs, coverage: i.coverage, cvText: i.cvText, archetypeMatch: i.student.archetypes[0]?.key === i.job.archetype });
  const result = await runAi({
    fn: "explainMatch",
    schema: ExplainOutput.transform((o) => o) as never,
    live: async () => {
      if (!i.cv) return fallback();
      const o = await generateStructured({
        tier: "smart", system: SYSTEM_EXPLAIN, schema: ExplainOutput, maxTokens: 6000,
        parts: [{ type: "text", text: `REQUIREMENTS:\n${reqs.map((r, n) => `${n}. [${r.importance}] ${r.text}`).join("\n")}\n\n${wrapJob(i.job.textLines.join("\n"))}\n\nCV:\n${JSON.stringify(stripPii(i.cv))}` }],
      });
      const rows: RequirementRow[] = reqs.map((r, n) => {
        const v = o.rows.find((x) => x.index === n);
        return { text: r.text, skill: r.skill, importance: r.importance, evidenceTier: r.evidenceTier, match: v?.match ?? "partial", cvEvidence: v?.cv_evidence ?? null };
      });
      return {
        rows, whyFit: o.why_fit,
        gaps: o.gaps.filter((g) => reqs[g.index]).map((g) => ({ requirement: reqs[g.index].text, blocking: g.blocking && ["critical", "high"].includes(reqs[g.index].importance), adjacentExperience: g.adjacent_experience, howToClose: g.how_to_close })),
      };
    },
    fallback,
  }) as Explanation;
  return { ...result, rows: orderRequirementRows(result.rows) };
}
```

`app/(app)/jobs/[id]/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { getActiveCv, getJob, getProfile, toStudent } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import { computeCoverage, cvSkillSource, jdRequirementSkills, profileSkillSource } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { scoreFit } from "@/lib/scoring/fit";
import { todayLondon } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";
import { explainMatch, type Explanation } from "@/lib/ai/explainMatch";
import { awardXP } from "@/lib/gamify/award";

export async function getExplanation(jobId: string): Promise<Explanation | { error: string }> {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const { data: cached } = await supabase.from("matches").select("explanation_json").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("job_id", jobId).maybeSingle();
  if (cached) return cached.explanation_json as Explanation;
  const [job, profile, cv] = await Promise.all([getJob(supabase, jobId), getProfile(supabase, user.id), getActiveCv(supabase, user.id)]);
  if (!job) return { error: "This role is no longer available." };
  const student = toStudent(profile);
  const coverage = computeCoverage(jdRequirementSkills(job.textLines, job.requirements), cv ? cvSkillSource(cv.parsed) : profileSkillSource(student.skills));
  try {
    const e = await explainMatch({ cv: cv?.parsed ?? null, cvText: cv?.rawText ?? "", student, job, coverage });
    await supabase.from("matches").upsert({ user_id: user.id, job_id: jobId, explanation_json: e });
    return e;
  } catch {
    return { error: "We couldn't build the explanation right now. Try again." };
  }
}

export async function markApplied(jobId: string) {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const [job, profile, cv] = await Promise.all([getJob(supabase, jobId), getProfile(supabase, user.id), getActiveCv(supabase, user.id)]);
  if (!job) return null;
  const student = toStudent(profile);
  const coverage = computeCoverage(jdRequirementSkills(job.textLines, job.requirements), cv ? cvSkillSource(cv.parsed) : profileSkillSource(student.skills));
  const fit = scoreFit(student, job, coverage, todayLondon());
  await supabase.from("applications").upsert({ user_id: user.id, job_id: jobId, status: "applied", updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: "user_id,job_id" });
  return awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: "applied", fitLabel: fit.label, jobTitle: job.title, deadline: job.deadline }, jobId);
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import type { Explanation } from "@/lib/ai/explainMatch";
import { getExplanation } from "./actions";

const ICON = { strong: "✓", partial: "~", missing: "✕" } as const;
const TONE = { strong: "text-[#0B6B49]", partial: "text-[#7A4100]", missing: "text-[#B42318]" } as const;

export function ExplainPanel({ jobId }: { jobId: string }) {
  const [e, setE] = useState<Explanation | { error: string } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => { getExplanation(jobId).then(setE); }, [jobId]);
  if (!e) return <div className="flex flex-col gap-2" role="status" aria-label="Loading explanation"><Skeleton className="h-5 w-2/3" /><Skeleton className="h-5 w-full" /><Skeleton className="h-5 w-5/6" /></div>;
  if ("error" in e) return <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{e.error}</p>;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {e.whyFit.length > 0 && <section><h3 className="font-bold">Why you fit</h3><ul className="list-disc pl-5 text-sm">{e.whyFit.map((w) => <li key={w}>{w}</li>)}</ul></section>}
      <section>
        <h3 className="font-bold">Requirements</h3>
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 text-sm">
          {e.rows.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.text} className="flex gap-2">
              <span className={`num-quest w-4 ${TONE[r.match]}`} aria-label={r.match}>{ICON[r.match]}</span>
              <span className="flex-1">{r.text} <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">· {r.importance.replace("_", " ")}</span>
                {r.cvEvidence && <span className="block text-xs italic text-muted-foreground">“{r.cvEvidence}”</span>}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>
      {e.gaps.length > 0 && <section><h3 className="font-bold">What&apos;s missing — and how to close it</h3>
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">{e.gaps.map((g) => (
          <li key={g.requirement} className="rounded-xl bg-[#F6F6FA] p-3"><b>{g.requirement}</b>{g.blocking && <span className="ml-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#B42318]">· important</span>}
            {g.adjacentExperience && <span className="block text-muted-foreground">Closest you have: {g.adjacentExperience}</span>}
            <span className="block">{g.howToClose}</span></li>))}</ul></section>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`app/(app)/jobs/[id]/apply-button.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { celebrate } from "@/components/reward-toast";
import { markApplied } from "./actions";

export function ApplyButton({ jobId, url, alreadyApplied }: { jobId: string; url: string; alreadyApplied: boolean }) {
  const awaiting = useRef(false);
  const [ask, setAsk] = useState(false);
  const [applied, setApplied] = useState(alreadyApplied);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  useEffect(() => {
    const onVis = () => { if (document.visibilityState === "visible" && awaiting.current) { awaiting.current = false; setAsk(true); } };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
  }, []);
  return (
    <>
      <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" onClick={() => { if (!applied) awaiting.current = true; }} className="btn-quest block py-3 text-center no-underline">
        {applied ? "Applied ✓ — open posting" : "Apply on company site ↗"}
      </a>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">You apply on the company&apos;s own site. Placed never submits anything for you.</p>
      <Dialog open={ask} onOpenChange={setAsk}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Did you apply?</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <button type="button" disabled={pending} className="btn-quest flex-1 py-3" onClick={() => start(async () => { celebrate(await markApplied(jobId)); setApplied(true); setAsk(false); })}>Yes, I applied</button>
            <button type="button" className="flex-1 rounded-[14px] border-2 py-3 font-bold" onClick={() => setAsk(false)}>Not yet</button>
          </div>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { getActiveCv, getJob, getProfile, toStudent } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import { computeCoverage, coverageLabel, cvSkillSource, jdRequirementSkills, profileSkillSource } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { scoreFit } from "@/lib/scoring/fit";
import { deadlineLabel, todayLondon } from "@/lib/scoring/dates";
import { FitBadge } from "@/components/fit-badge";
import { SaveButton } from "@/components/save-button";
import { ExplainPanel } from "./explain-panel";
import { ApplyButton } from "./apply-button";

export default async function JobPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const [job, profile, cv, { data: app }] = await Promise.all([
    getJob(supabase, id), getProfile(supabase, user.id), getActiveCv(supabase, user.id),
    supabase.from("applications").select("status").eq("user_id", user.id).eq("job_id", id).maybeSingle(),
  ]);
  if (!job) notFound();
  const today = todayLondon();
  const student = toStudent(profile);
  const coverage = computeCoverage(jdRequirementSkills(job.textLines, job.requirements), cv ? cvSkillSource(cv.parsed) : profileSkillSource(student.skills));
  const fit = scoreFit(student, job, coverage, today);

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_400px]">
      <article className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[20px] border bg-white p-6">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-muted-foreground">{job.company} · {job.location || "Location not stated"} · {deadlineLabel(job.deadline, today)}</p>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">{job.title}</h1>
        {job.summary && <p className="text-muted-foreground">{job.summary}</p>}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-[15px] leading-relaxed">
          {job.textLines.map((l, i) => l.startsWith("## ")
            ? <h2 key={i} className="mt-3 text-lg font-bold">{l.slice(3)}</h2>
            : l.startsWith("- ") ? <p key={i} className="pl-4 before:-ml-4 before:mr-2 before:content-['•']">{l.slice(2)}</p>
            : <p key={i}>{l}</p>)}
        </div>
      </article>
      <aside className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:sticky lg:top-6 lg:self-start">
        <section className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[20px] border bg-white p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><FitBadge label={fit.label} score={fit.global} /><SaveButton jobId={job.id} initiallySaved={Boolean(app)} /></div>
          {fit.gates.filter((g) => !g.passed).map((g) => <p key={g.key} className="rounded-xl bg-[#FFF1DC] px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#7A4100]">{g.reason}</p>)}
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{coverageLabel(coverage)}</p>
          <ExplainPanel jobId={job.id} />
        </section>
        {/* Task 23 inserts <TailorPanel /> here */}
        <section className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-[20px] border bg-white p-5">
          <ApplyButton jobId={job.id} url={job.url} alreadyApplied={app?.status !== undefined && app.status !== "saved"} />
        </section>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
    { id: "x0-b1", text: "Used Python to analyse ticket sales" },
  ] }],
  projects: [], leadership: [], skills: [{ group: "Skills", items: ["Python", "Excel"] }], interests: null, sectionOrder: [], yearGuess: null, archetypes: [],
};
const sourceText = "Organised weekly society events for 60 members\nUsed Python to analyse ticket sales\nPython, Excel";
const base = (t: TailoredCV) => ({ source, sourceText, confirmedFacts: [] as string[], tailored: t, jdKeywords: ["stakeholder", "events"], coverageBefore: 0.5, coverageAfter: 0.6 });

function withBullet(text: string, sourceRefs = ["x0-b1"], extra: Partial<TailoredCV> = {}): TailoredCV {
  const t = toTailored(source);
  t.experience[0].bullets[1] = { id: "x0-b1", text, sourceRefs };
  return { ...t, ...extra };
}

describe("verifyTailoring", () => {
  it("passes an honest reorder/rephrase", () => {
    const t = withBullet("Analysed ticket sales using Python");
    expect(verifyTailoring(base(t)).verdict).toBe("pass");
  });
  it("blocks invented metrics, unless the student confirmed them", () => {
    const t = withBullet("Used Python to analyse ticket sales, increasing revenue by 40%");
    const r = verifyTailoring(base(t));
    expect(r.verdict).toBe("block");
    expect(r.issues.find((i) => i.type === "inventedMetrics")!.claim).toBe("40%");
    expect(verifyTailoring({ ...base(t), confirmedFacts: ["Ticket revenue grew 40%"] }).issues.some((i) => i.type === "inventedMetrics")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("blocks used → built authorship escalation", () => {
    const r = verifyTailoring(base(withBullet("Built a Python pipeline to analyse ticket sales")));
    expect(r.issues.some((i) => i.type === "authorshipEscalation" && i.severity === "block")).toBe(true);
  });
  it("blocks injected gap skills in bullets or the skills list", () => {
    const t = withBullet("Used Python and Tableau to analyse ticket sales");
    expect(verifyTailoring(base(t)).issues.find((i) => i.type === "skillInjection")!.claim).toBe("Tableau");
    const t2 = toTailored(source); t2.skills = [{ group: "Skills", items: ["Python", "Excel", "SQL"] }];
    expect(verifyTailoring(base(t2)).verdict).toBe("block");
  });
  it("blocks orphan bullets and forbidden phrases", () => {
    expect(verifyTailoring(base(withBullet("Analysed sales", []))).issues.some((i) => i.type === "orphanBullets")).toBe(true);
    expect(verifyTailoring(base(withBullet("Proven track record of analysing ticket sales with Python"))).issues.some((i) => i.type === "forbiddenPhrases")).toBe(true);
  });
  it("blocks changed employers/titles not in the source", () => {
    const t = toTailored(source); t.experience[0] = { ...t.experience[0], title: "Head of Events" };
    expect(verifyTailoring(base(t)).issues.some((i) => i.type === "unsupportedFacts")).toBe(true);
  });
  it("warns (does not block) when a real number migrates between bullets", () => {
    const r = verifyTailoring(base(withBullet("Used Python to analyse ticket sales for 60 members")));
    expect(r.verdict).toBe("warn");
    expect(r.issues[0].type).toBe("perBulletDrift");
  });
  it("warns when coverage did not improve", () => {
    expect(verifyTailoring({ ...base(toTailored(source)), coverageAfter: 0.5 }).issues.some((i) => i.type === "coverage")).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("revertBlocked", () => {
  it("restores offending bullets to the original text and removes injected skills", () => {
    const t = withBullet("Built a Tableau dashboard to analyse ticket sales");
    t.skills = [{ group: "Skills", items: ["Python", "Excel", "Tableau"] }];
    const r = verifyTailoring(base(t));
    const { tailored, reverted } = revertBlocked(t, source, r.issues);
    expect(tailored.experience[0].bullets[1].text).toBe("Used Python to analyse ticket sales");
    expect(tailored.skills[0].items).not.toContain("Tableau");
    expect(reverted.map((x) => x.bulletId)).toContain("x0-b1");
    expect(verifyTailoring(base(tailored)).verdict).not.toBe("block");
  });
});
```

`lib/verify/tailoring.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, TailoredCV, TailoredEntry } from "@/lib/types";
import { extractSkills } from "@/lib/skills/extract";

export type IssueType = "inventedMetrics" | "unsupportedFacts" | "authorshipEscalation" | "skillInjection" | "orphanBullets" | "perBulletDrift" | "forbiddenPhrases" | "coverage";
export type Issue = { type: IssueType; severity: "block" | "warn"; bulletId?: string; entryId?: string; claim: string; reason: string };
export type VerifyReport = { verdict: "pass" | "warn" | "block"; issues: Issue[] };

const NUM = /\d+(?:[.,]\d+)*(?:\s?(?:%|k\b|m\b|bn\b|x\b))?/gi;
const BUILD = /\b(built|developed|designed|architected|engineered|created|implemented|launched|founded|led)\b/gi;
const WEAK = /\b(used|using|familiar with|assisted|helped|supported|contributed to|participated in|exposure to|member)\b/i;
const FORBIDDEN = ["proven track record", "world-class", "world class", "rockstar", "ninja", "guru", "thought leader", "results-driven", "synergy"];
const WARN_PHRASES = ["passionate about", "dynamic", "detail-oriented"];
const STOP = new Set("with from that this have into their about using used over under while also more most such than then they them were will your which within across".split(" "));

const nums = (t: string) => new Set((t.match(NUM) ?? []).map((n) => n.replace(/[,\s]/g, "").toLowerCase()));
const norm = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ").trim();
const entriesOf = (c: { experience: TailoredEntry[] | ParsedCV["experience"]; projects: unknown[]; leadership: unknown[] }) =>
  [...c.experience, ...c.projects, ...c.leadership] as { id: string; org: string; title: string; bullets: { id: string; text: string; sourceRefs?: string[] }[] }[];

export function toTailored(cv: ParsedCV): TailoredCV {
  const map = (es: ParsedCV["experience"]) => es.map((e) => ({ ...e, bullets: e.bullets.map((b) => ({ ...b, sourceRefs: [b.id] })) }));
  return { summary: cv.summary, education: cv.education, experience: map(cv.experience), projects: map(cv.projects), leadership: map(cv.leadership), skills: cv.skills, interests: cv.interests, changes: [] };
}

function sourceBulletMap(cv: ParsedCV) {
  const m = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const e of entriesOf(cv)) for (const b of e.bullets) m.set(b.id, b.text);
  for (const e of cv.education) for (const d of e.details) m.set(d.id, d.text);
  return m;
}

export function verifyTailoring(i: { source: ParsedCV; sourceText: string; confirmedFacts: string[]; tailored: TailoredCV; jdKeywords: string[]; coverageBefore: number | null; coverageAfter: number | null }): VerifyReport {
  const issues: Issue[] = [];
  const srcBullets = sourceBulletMap(i.source);
  const srcAll = [i.sourceText, JSON.stringify(i.source), ...i.confirmedFacts].join("\n");
  const srcNums = nums(srcAll);
  const srcSkills = new Set(extractSkills(srcAll));
  const srcEntries = new Map(entriesOf(i.source).map((e) => [e.id, e]));
  const jd = new Set(i.jdKeywords.map((k) => k.toLowerCase()));

  for (const e of entriesOf(i.tailored)) {
    const se = srcEntries.get(e.id);
    for (const field of ["org", "title"] as const) {
      const v = e[field];
      if (v && (!se || norm(se[field]) !== norm(v)) && !norm(srcAll).includes(norm(v))) {
        issues.push({ type: "unsupportedFacts", severity: "block", entryId: e.id, claim: v, reason: `“${v}” does not appear in your original CV` });
      }
    }
    for (const b of e.bullets) {
      const refs = b.sourceRefs ?? [];
      const srcText = refs.map((r) => srcBullets.get(r)).filter(Boolean).join(" ");
      if (refs.length === 0 || refs.some((r) => !srcBullets.has(r))) {
        issues.push({ type: "orphanBullets", severity: "block", bulletId: b.id, claim: b.text, reason: "This bullet isn't linked to anything in your original CV" });
        continue;
      }
      for (const n of nums(b.text)) {
        if (!srcNums.has(n)) issues.push({ type: "inventedMetrics", severity: "block", bulletId: b.id, claim: (b.text.match(NUM) ?? []).find((x) => x.replace(/[,\s]/g, "").toLowerCase() === n) ?? n, reason: "This number isn't in your CV — confirm it or we'll leave it out" });
        else if (!nums(srcText).has(n)) issues.push({ type: "perBulletDrift", severity: "warn", bulletId: b.id, claim: n, reason: "This number came from a different bullet" });
      }
      const built = [...b.text.matchAll(BUILD)].map((m) => m[1].toLowerCase()).filter((v) => !new RegExp(`\\b${v}\\b`, "i").test(srcText));
      if (built.length && WEAK.test(srcText)) issues.push({ type: "authorshipEscalation", severity: "block", bulletId: b.id, claim: built[0], reason: `Your CV says you used/helped with this — “${built[0]}” overstates your role` });
      const lower = b.text.toLowerCase();
      for (const p of FORBIDDEN) if (lower.includes(p)) issues.push({ type: "forbiddenPhrases", severity: "block", bulletId: b.id, claim: p, reason: "Empty buzzword recruiters discount" });
      if (lower.includes("expert in") && !/expert/i.test(srcText)) issues.push({ type: "forbiddenPhrases", severity: "block", bulletId: b.id, claim: "expert in", reason: "Your CV doesn't claim expertise" });
      for (const p of WARN_PHRASES) if (lower.includes(p)) issues.push({ type: "forbiddenPhrases", severity: "warn", bulletId: b.id, claim: p, reason: "Generic phrase — show it instead" });
      const srcWords = new Set(norm(srcText).split(" "));
      const newWords = norm(b.text).split(" ").filter((w) => w.length >= 4 && !STOP.has(w) && !srcWords.has(w) && !jd.has(w));
      if (newWords.length > 6) issues.push({ type: "perBulletDrift", severity: "warn", bulletId: b.id, claim: newWords.slice(0, 4).join(", "), reason: "Lots of new wording — check it still says what you did" });
    }
  }

  const tailoredText = [i.tailored.summary ?? "", ...entriesOf(i.tailored).flatMap((e) => e.bullets.map((b) => b.text)), ...i.tailored.skills.flatMap((g) => g.items)].join("\n");
  for (const s of extractSkills(tailoredText)) {
    if (!srcSkills.has(s)) issues.push({ type: "skillInjection", severity: "block", claim: s, reason: `${s} isn't anywhere in your CV — we never add skills you don't have` });
  }
  if (i.coverageBefore !== null && i.coverageAfter !== null && i.coverageAfter <= i.coverageBefore) {
    issues.push({ type: "coverage", severity: "warn", claim: `${Math.round(i.coverageBefore * 100)}% → ${Math.round(i.coverageAfter * 100)}%`, reason: "Tailoring didn't improve keyword coverage" });
  }
  const verdict = issues.some((x) => x.severity === "block") ? "block" : issues.length ? "warn" : "pass";
  return { verdict, issues };
}

export function revertBlocked(tailored: TailoredCV, source: ParsedCV, issues: Issue[]) {
  const src = sourceBulletMap(source);
  const srcEntries = new Map(entriesOf(source).map((e) => [e.id, e]));
  const blocks = issues.filter((x) => x.severity === "block");
  const injected = new Set(blocks.filter((x) => x.type === "skillInjection").map((x) => x.claim.toLowerCase()));
  const badBullets = new Set(blocks.filter((x) => x.bulletId).map((x) => x.bulletId!));
  const badEntries = new Set(blocks.filter((x) => x.entryId).map((x) => x.entryId!));
  const reverted: { bulletId: string; reason: string }[] = [];

  const fixEntries = (es: TailoredEntry[]): TailoredEntry[] => es.map((e) => {
    const se = srcEntries.get(e.id);
    const head = badEntries.has(e.id) && se ? { org: se.org, title: se.title } : {};
    const bullets = e.bullets.flatMap((b) => {
      const mentionsInjected = extractSkills(b.text).some((s) => injected.has(s.toLowerCase()));
      if (!badBullets.has(b.id) && !mentionsInjected) return [b];
      const reason = blocks.find((x) => x.bulletId === b.id)?.reason ?? "Mentioned a skill that isn't in your CV";
      const original = b.sourceRefs.map((r) => src.get(r)).find(Boolean);
      reverted.push({ bulletId: b.id, reason });
      return original ? [{ id: b.id, text: original, sourceRefs: [b.sourceRefs[0]] }] : [];
    });
    return { ...e, ...head, bullets };
  });

  const out: TailoredCV = {
    ...tailored,
    experience: fixEntries(tailored.experience), projects: fixEntries(tailored.projects), leadership: fixEntries(tailored.leadership),
    skills: tailored.skills.map((g) => ({ ...g, items: g.items.filter((it) => !injected.has((extractSkills(it)[0] ?? it).toLowerCase())) })),
    summary: tailored.summary && extractSkills(tailored.summary).some((s) => injected.has(s.toLowerCase())) ? source.summary : tailored.summary,
    changes: tailored.changes.filter((c) => !reverted.some((r) => r.bulletId === c.bulletId)),
  };
  return { tailored: out, reverted };
}
```

```ts
import type { TailoredCV, TailoredEntry } from "@/lib/types";
import { toTailored } from "@/lib/verify/tailoring";

export function heuristicTailor(i: { cv: ParsedCV; allowed: string[]; supported: string[] }): TailoredCV {
  const t = toTailored(i.cv);
  const allowed = i.allowed.map((s) => s.toLowerCase());
  const hits = (text: string) => extractSkills(text).filter((s) => allowed.includes(s.toLowerCase()));
  const changes: TailoredCV["changes"] = [];
  const reorder = (es: TailoredEntry[]) => es.map((e) => {
    const sorted = [...e.bullets].sort((a, b) => hits(b.text).length - hits(a.text).length);
    if (sorted[0] && sorted[0].id !== e.bullets[0]?.id) {
      changes.push({ bulletId: sorted[0].id, before: sorted[0].text, after: sorted[0].text, reason: `Moved up: mentions ${hits(sorted[0].text).join(", ")}, which this role asks for`, jdRequirement: hits(sorted[0].text)[0] ?? null, keywordsAdded: [] });
    }
    return { ...e, bullets: sorted };
  });
  t.experience = reorder(t.experience); t.projects = reorder(t.projects); t.leadership = reorder(t.leadership);
  const listed = new Set(t.skills.flatMap((g) => g.items.map((x) => x.toLowerCase())));
  const surface = i.supported.filter((s) => !listed.has(s.toLowerCase()));
  if (surface.length) {
    t.skills = t.skills.length ? t.skills.map((g, n) => (n === 0 ? { ...g, items: [...g.items, ...surface] } : g)) : [{ group: "Skills", items: surface }];
    changes.push({ bulletId: "skills", before: "", after: surface.join(", "), reason: `Listed ${surface.join(", ")} in Skills — your CV already mentions ${surface.length > 1 ? "them" : "it"} elsewhere`, jdRequirement: surface[0], keywordsAdded: surface });
  }
  t.skills = t.skills.map((g) => ({ ...g, items: [...g.items].sort((a, b) => Number(allowed.includes(b.toLowerCase())) - Number(allowed.includes(a.toLowerCase()))) }));
  t.changes = changes;
  return t;
}
```

Append to `lib/ai/prompts.ts`:

```ts
export const SYSTEM_TAILOR = `You tailor a UK student's CV to one internship posting. Keywords get reformulated, never fabricated.
${UNTRUSTED_JD_RULE}
ALLOWED: reorder bullets and sections; rephrase a bullet so the posting's wording is visible IF the original bullet already shows it; move a skill already mentioned anywhere in the CV into the Skills list; tighten wording.
FORBIDDEN: adding any skill, tool, number, employer, job title, responsibility or result not in the original; turning "used/helped/member" into "built/led/developed"; buzzwords ("proven track record", "world-class", "expert in").
Use ONLY skills from the ALLOWED KEYWORDS list when adding keywords.
Keep every bullet's id; set sourceRefs to the original bullet id(s) it is derived from. Never create a bullet without sourceRefs.
For every changed bullet add a changes[] entry: before, after, reason (which posting requirement it serves, in plain words), jdRequirement, keywordsAdded.
Keep entries' org/title/dates exactly as in the original.`;
```

`lib/ai/tailorCV.ts`:

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, TailoredCV } from "@/lib/types";
import type { JobListItem } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import { stripPii } from "@/lib/cv-score/pii";
import { generateStructured, runAi } from "./provider";
import { SYSTEM_TAILOR, wrapJob } from "./prompts";
import { TailoredCvOutput } from "./schemas";
import { heuristicTailor } from "./heuristics";

export async function tailorCV(i: { cv: ParsedCV; job: JobListItem; allowed: string[]; supported: string[]; feedback: string[] | null }): Promise<TailoredCV> {
  return runAi({
    fn: "tailorCV",
    schema: TailoredCvOutput as never,
    live: async () => {
      const o = await generateStructured({
        tier: "smart", system: SYSTEM_TAILOR, schema: TailoredCvOutput, maxTokens: 16000,
        parts: [{ type: "text", text:
          `ALLOWED KEYWORDS: ${i.allowed.join(", ") || "(none)"}\n\n${wrapJob(`${i.job.title}\n${i.job.textLines.join("\n")}`)}\n\nORIGINAL CV:\n${JSON.stringify(stripPii(i.cv))}` +
          (i.feedback ? `\n\nYOUR PREVIOUS ATTEMPT WAS REJECTED. Remove or restate from the original these claims: ${i.feedback.join("; ")}` : "") }],
      });
      return { ...o, education: i.cv.education, interests: i.cv.interests } as TailoredCV;
    },
    fallback: () => heuristicTailor({ cv: i.cv, allowed: i.allowed, supported: i.supported }),
  }) as Promise<TailoredCV>;
}
```

```ts
import type { ParsedCV, TailoredCV } from "@/lib/types";
import type { JobListItem } from "@/lib/db/queries";
import { computeCoverage, jdRequirementSkills, type CvSkillSource } from "@/lib/scoring/coverage";
import { canonicalize } from "@/lib/skills/extract";
import { revertBlocked, verifyTailoring, toTailored, type VerifyReport } from "@/lib/verify/tailoring";
import type { tailorCV as TailorFn } from "@/lib/ai/tailorCV";

export type TailoredFor = { requirement: string; skill: string | null; state: "covered" | "surfaced" | "missing" };
export type TailorOutcome = {
  tailored: TailoredCV; report: VerifyReport; coverageBefore: number | null; coverageAfter: number | null;
  tailoredFor: TailoredFor[]; reverted: { bulletId: string; reason: string }[]; unconfirmedNumbers: string[];
};

function tailoredSource(t: TailoredCV): CvSkillSource {
  const es = [...t.experience, ...t.projects, ...t.leadership];
  return {
    skillsList: t.skills.flatMap((g) => g.items.map((i) => canonicalize(i) ?? i)),
    bulletText: es.flatMap((e) => [e.title, ...e.bullets.map((b) => b.text)]).join("\n"),
    otherText: [t.summary ?? "", t.interests ?? "", ...t.education.flatMap((e) => [e.degree, ...e.details.map((d) => d.text)])].join("\n"),
  };
}

export async function runTailoring(i: { cv: ParsedCV; rawText: string; job: JobListItem; confirmedFacts: string[]; tailor: typeof TailorFn }): Promise<TailorOutcome> {
  const reqs = jdRequirementSkills(i.job.textLines, i.job.requirements);
  const before = computeCoverage(reqs, tailoredSource(toTailored(i.cv)));
  const allowed = before.items.filter((x) => x.status !== "gap").map((x) => x.skill);
  const supported = before.items.filter((x) => x.status === "supportedByResume").map((x) => x.skill);
  const jdKeywords = reqs.map((r) => r.skill);
  const verify = (t: TailoredCV) => verifyTailoring({
    source: i.cv, sourceText: i.rawText, confirmedFacts: i.confirmedFacts, tailored: t, jdKeywords,
    coverageBefore: before.weighted, coverageAfter: computeCoverage(reqs, tailoredSource(t)).weighted,
  });

  let tailored = await i.tailor({ cv: i.cv, job: i.job, allowed, supported, feedback: null });
  let report = verify(tailored);
  const firstIssues = report.issues;
  if (report.verdict === "block") {
    tailored = await i.tailor({ cv: i.cv, job: i.job, allowed, supported, feedback: report.issues.filter((x) => x.severity === "block").map((x) => x.claim) });
    report = verify(tailored);
  }
  let reverted: TailorOutcome["reverted"] = [];
  if (report.verdict === "block") {
    const r = revertBlocked(tailored, i.cv, report.issues);
    tailored = r.tailored; reverted = r.reverted; report = verify(tailored);
    if (report.verdict === "block") { tailored = toTailored(i.cv); report = verify(tailored); }
  }
  const after = computeCoverage(reqs, tailoredSource(tailored));
  const afterStatus = new Map(after.items.map((x) => [x.skill, x.status]));
  const tailoredFor: TailoredFor[] = before.items.slice(0, 8).map((b) => ({
    requirement: b.skill, skill: b.skill,
    state: b.status === "gap" ? "missing" : b.status === "supportedByResume" && afterStatus.get(b.skill) === "existing" ? "surfaced" : "covered",
  }));
  const unconfirmedNumbers = [...new Set(firstIssues.filter((x) => x.type === "inventedMetrics").map((x) => x.claim))];
  return { tailored, report, coverageBefore: before.weighted, coverageAfter: after.weighted, tailoredFor, reverted, unconfirmedNumbers };
}
```

Run `npx vitest run tests/tailor` → PASS. (In the test, `allowed` = Excel and Stata; the fake model injects Tableau, which is blocked twice and then reverted. Stata becomes "existing" through the Skills list, because Stata is in the source education details, so it is not an injection.)

`lib/pdf/cv-document.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Document, Page, Text, View, StyleSheet, renderToBuffer } from "@react-pdf/renderer";
import type { ParsedCV, TailoredCV, TailoredEntry } from "@/lib/types";

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  page: { padding: 36, fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "Helvetica", lineHeight: 1.35 },
  name: { fontSize: 18, fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold" },
  contact: { marginTop: 2, marginBottom: 8, color: "#333" },
  h: { fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold", marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 3, borderBottom: "1pt solid #000", paddingBottom: 1, textTransform: "uppercase" },
  row: { flexDirection: "row", justifyContent: "space-between" },
  bold: { fontFamily: "Helvetica-Bold" },
  bullet: { flexDirection: "row", marginLeft: 8 },
});

function Entries({ title, items }: { title: string; items: TailoredEntry[] }) {
  if (!items.length) return null;
  return (
    <View>
      <Text style={s.h}>{title}</Text>
      {items.map((e) => (
        <View key={e.id} wrap={false} style={{ marginBottom: 4 }}>
          <View style={s.row}><Text style={s.bold}>{[e.org, e.title].filter(Boolean).join(" — ")}</Text><Text>{e.dates}</Text></View>
          {e.bullets.map((b) => <View key={b.id} style={s.bullet}><Text>• </Text><Text style={{ flex: 1 }}>{b.text}</Text></View>)}
        </View>
      ))}
    </View>
  );
}

export function renderCvPdf(cv: TailoredCV, contact: Pick<ParsedCV, "name" | "email" | "phone" | "location" | "links">): Promise<Buffer> {
  const doc = (
    <Document title={`${contact.name} CV`}>
      <Page size="A4" style={s.page}>
        <Text style={s.name}>{contact.name}</Text>
        <Text style={s.contact}>{[contact.email, contact.phone, contact.location, ...contact.links].filter(Boolean).join("  |  ")}</Text>
        {cv.summary && <Text>{cv.summary}</Text>}
        {cv.education.length > 0 && <View><Text style={s.h}>Education</Text>
          {cv.education.map((e) => <View key={e.id} style={{ marginBottom: 4 }}>
            <View style={s.row}><Text style={s.bold}>{e.institution}</Text><Text>{e.dates}</Text></View>
            <Text>{[e.degree, e.grade].filter(Boolean).join(" — ")}</Text>
            {e.details.map((d) => <View key={d.id} style={s.bullet}><Text>• </Text><Text style={{ flex: 1 }}>{d.text}</Text></View>)}
          </View>)}</View>}
        <Entries title="Experience" items={cv.experience} />
        <Entries title="Projects" items={cv.projects} />
        <Entries title="Leadership & Activities" items={cv.leadership} />
        {cv.skills.length > 0 && <View><Text style={s.h}>Skills</Text>{cv.skills.map((g) => <Text key={g.group}><Text style={s.bold}>{g.group}: </Text>{g.items.join(", ")}</Text>)}</View>}
        {cv.interests && <View><Text style={s.h}>Interests</Text><Text>{cv.interests}</Text></View>}
      </Page>
    </Document>
  );
  return renderToBuffer(doc);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Server actions + download route**

Append to `app/(app)/jobs/[id]/actions.ts`:

```ts
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { runTailoring } from "@/lib/tailor/run";
import { tailorCV } from "@/lib/ai/tailorCV";
import { renderCvPdf } from "@/lib/pdf/cv-document";

export async function tailorForJob(jobId: string) {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const [job, cv, { data: facts }] = await Promise.all([
    getJob(supabase, jobId), getActiveCv(supabase, user.id), supabase.from("confirmed_facts").select("claim").eq("user_id", user.id),
  ]);
  if (!job) return { error: "This role is no longer available." };
  if (!cv) return { error: "Upload your CV first — tailoring needs it." };
  let outcome;
  try {
    outcome = await runTailoring({ cv: cv.parsed, rawText: cv.rawText, job, confirmedFacts: (facts ?? []).map((f) => f.claim), tailor: tailorCV });
  } catch {
    return { error: "Tailoring failed this time — try again in a minute." };
  }
  if (outcome.report.verdict === "block") return { error: "We couldn't produce a version we can stand behind. Your original CV is unchanged." };
  const pdf = await renderCvPdf(outcome.tailored, cv.parsed);
  const path = `${user.id}/${jobId}.pdf`;
  await createAdminClient().storage.from("tailored").upload(path, pdf, { contentType: "application/pdf", upsert: true });
  await supabase.from("applications").upsert({
    user_id: user.id, job_id: jobId, tailored_json: outcome.tailored, verify_report: outcome.report, tailored_cv_path: path, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  }, { onConflict: "user_id,job_id" });
  const award = await awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: "cv_tailored" }, jobId);
  return { outcome, award, pdfUrl: `/api/tailored/${jobId}` };
}

export async function confirmNumber(jobId: string, claim: string, choice: "confirm" | "edit" | "remove" | "keep_original", edited?: string) {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  if (choice === "confirm" || (choice === "edit" && edited?.trim())) {
    await supabase.from("confirmed_facts").insert({ user_id: user.id, claim: choice === "confirm" ? claim : edited!.trim() });
    return tailorForJob(jobId);
  }
  return null; // remove / keep original: the reverted version already stands
}
```

The `applications` upsert in `tailorForJob` must not overwrite `status` on an existing row, which is why `status` is left out. On a new row, the column default `saved` applies.

`app/api/tailored/[jobId]/route.ts`:

```ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";

export async function GET(request: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ jobId: string }> }) {
  const { jobId } = await params;
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: auth } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!auth.user) return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/login", request.url));
  const { data: app } = await supabase.from("applications").select("tailored_cv_path").eq("user_id", auth.user.id).eq("job_id", jobId).maybeSingle();
  if (!app?.tailored_cv_path) return NextResponse.json({ error: "No tailored CV yet" }, { status: 404 });
  const { data } = await createAdminClient().storage.from("tailored").createSignedUrl(app.tailored_cv_path, 60, { download: "CV-tailored.pdf" });
  return data ? NextResponse.redirect(data.signedUrl) : NextResponse.json({ error: "Unavailable" }, { status: 500 });
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { motion } from "motion/react";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { celebrate } from "@/components/reward-toast";
import type { TailorOutcome } from "@/lib/tailor/run";
import { confirmNumber, tailorForJob } from "./actions";

const STATE = {
  covered: { icon: "✓", label: "Already on your CV", tone: "text-[#0B6B49]" },
  surfaced: { icon: "↻", label: "Made visible from your experience", tone: "text-[#2340A8]" },
  missing: { icon: "✕", label: "Not in your CV — we didn't invent it", tone: "text-[#B42318]" },
} as const;

export function TailorPanel({ jobId, hasCv }: { jobId: string; hasCv: boolean }) {
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const [res, setRes] = useState<{ outcome: TailorOutcome; pdfUrl: string } | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [ask, setAsk] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [edit, setEdit] = useState("");

  const run = () => start(async () => {
    setError(null);
    const r = await tailorForJob(jobId);
    if ("error" in r) { setError(r.error ?? "Something went wrong"); return; }
    setRes(r); celebrate(r.award);
    if (r.outcome.unconfirmedNumbers.length) setAsk(r.outcome.unconfirmedNumbers[0]);
  });
  const answer = (choice: "confirm" | "edit" | "remove" | "keep_original") => start(async () => {
    const r = await confirmNumber(jobId, ask!, choice, edit);
    setAsk(null);
    if (r && "outcome" in r) setRes(r);
  });

  if (!hasCv) return <section className="rounded-[20px] border bg-white p-5"><p className="font-bold">Tailor your CV</p><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Upload a CV to tailor it to this role.</p></section>;
  const pct = (x: number | null) => (x === null ? "–" : `${Math.round(x * 100)}%`);

  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-[20px] border bg-white p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><h2 className="font-bold">Tailor my CV</h2><span className="chip-xp">+30 XP</span></div>
      {!res && <button type="button" onClick={run} disabled={pending} className="btn-quest py-3">{pending ? "Tailoring honestly…" : "Tailor my CV for this role"}</button>}
      {error && <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>}
      {res && (
        <>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="num-quest text-2xl text-muted-foreground">{pct(res.outcome.coverageBefore)}</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">→</span>
            <motion.span initial={{ scale: 0.6, opacity: 0 }} animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }} className="num-quest text-4xl text-primary">{pct(res.outcome.coverageAfter)}</motion.span>
            <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">keyword coverage</span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="mb-1.5 text-sm font-bold">What we tailored for</h3>
            <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">{res.outcome.tailoredFor.map((t) => (
              <li key={t.requirement} className="flex gap-2"><span className={`num-quest w-4 ${STATE[t.state].tone}`} aria-hidden="true">{STATE[t.state].icon}</span><span><b>{t.requirement}</b> · {STATE[t.state].label}</span></li>))}</ul>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="mb-1.5 text-sm font-bold">What changed, and why</h3>
            <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">{res.outcome.tailored.changes.map((c) => (
              <li key={c.bulletId + c.after} className="rounded-xl bg-[#F6F6FA] p-3">
                {c.before && c.before !== c.after && <p className="text-muted-foreground line-through">{c.before}</p>}
                <p className="font-semibold">{c.after}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Why: {c.reason}</p>
              </li>))}
              {res.outcome.tailored.changes.length === 0 && <li className="text-muted-foreground">Your CV already covers this role well — no changes needed.</li>}</ul>
          </div>
          {res.outcome.reverted.length > 0 && <div className="rounded-xl bg-[#FFF1DC] p-3 text-sm"><b>Reverted — not supported by your CV:</b><ul className="list-disc pl-5">{res.outcome.reverted.map((r) => <li key={r.bulletId}>{r.reason}</li>)}</ul></div>}
          {res.outcome.report.verdict !== "block" && <p className="rounded-xl bg-[#E3F6EE] px-3 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-[#0B6B49]">✓ Every claim traced to your original CV</p>}
          <a href={res.pdfUrl} className="btn-quest block py-3 text-center no-underline">Download tailored PDF</a>
        </>
      )}
      <Dialog open={ask !== null} onOpenChange={(o) => !o && setAsk(null)}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Is “{ask}” a real number from your experience?</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">We left it out because it isn&apos;t in your CV. Only keep it if it&apos;s true.</p>
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <button type="button" className="btn-quest py-2.5" onClick={() => answer("confirm")}>Yes, it&apos;s accurate</button>
            <div className="flex gap-2"><input aria-label="Correct number" value={edit} onChange={(e) => setEdit(e.target.value)} placeholder="The real figure" className="flex-1 rounded-xl border px-3" />
              <button type="button" className="rounded-xl border-2 px-3 font-bold" onClick={() => answer("edit")}>Use this</button></div>
            <button type="button" className="rounded-xl border-2 py-2.5 font-bold" onClick={() => answer("remove")}>Remove it</button>
            <button type="button" className="rounded-xl border-2 py-2.5 font-bold" onClick={() => answer("keep_original")}>Keep my original wording</button>
          </div>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Status action**

`app/(app)/tracker/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { awardXP } from "@/lib/gamify/award";
import type { AppStatus } from "@/lib/types";

const EVENT = { applied: "applied", interview: "interview", rejected: "rejected", offer: "offer" } as const;

export async function updateStatus(appId: string, status: AppStatus) {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const { data: app } = await supabase.from("applications").update({ status, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq("id", appId).eq("user_id", user.id).select("job_id, jobs(title, deadline)").single();
  revalidatePath("/tracker");
  if (!app || status === "saved") return null;
  const job = app.jobs as unknown as { title: string; deadline: string | null } | null;
  return awardXP(supabase, user.id, { type: EVENT[status], jobTitle: job?.title, deadline: job?.deadline }, app.job_id);
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useOptimistic, useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import type { AppStatus } from "@/lib/types";
import { celebrate } from "@/components/reward-toast";
import { updateStatus } from "./actions";

export type Card = { id: string; jobId: string; title: string; company: string; status: AppStatus };
const COLUMNS: { key: AppStatus; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "saved", label: "Saved" }, { key: "applied", label: "Applied" }, { key: "interview", label: "Interview" },
  { key: "offer", label: "Offer" }, { key: "rejected", label: "Rejected" },
];

export function Board({ cards }: { cards: Card[] }) {
  const [, start] = useTransition();
  const [optimistic, move] = useOptimistic(cards, (s, { id, status }: { id: string; status: AppStatus }) => s.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, status } : c)));
  const change = (id: string, status: AppStatus) => start(async () => { move({ id, status }); celebrate(await updateStatus(id, status)); });

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-5">
      {COLUMNS.map((col) => (
        <section key={col.key} aria-label={col.label} className="flex min-h-48 flex-col gap-2 rounded-[18px] bg-[#EFEFF5] p-3"
          onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()} onDrop={(e) => change(e.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain"), col.key)}>
          <h2 className="px-1 text-sm font-bold">{col.label} <span className="text-muted-foreground">{optimistic.filter((c) => c.status === col.key).length}</span></h2>
          {col.key === "rejected" && <p className="px-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Rejection is part of the game — +20 XP for logging it.</p>}
          {optimistic.filter((c) => c.status === col.key).map((c) => (
            <article key={c.id} draggable onDragStart={(e) => e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", c.id)} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border bg-white p-3">
              <Link href={`/jobs/${c.jobId}`} className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground no-underline">{c.title}</Link>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.company}</span>
              <label className="sr-only" htmlFor={`s-${c.id}`}>Status</label>
              <select id={`s-${c.id}`} value={c.status} onChange={(e) => change(c.id, e.target.value as AppStatus)} className="rounded-lg border px-2 py-1 text-xs">
                {COLUMNS.map((o) => <option key={o.key} value={o.key}>{o.label}</option>)}
              </select>
            </article>
          ))}
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`app/(app)/tracker/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { Board, type Card } from "./board";

export default async function Tracker() {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const { data } = await supabase.from("applications").select("id, job_id, status, jobs(title, companies(name))").eq("user_id", user.id).order("updated_at", { ascending: false });
  const cards: Card[] = (data ?? []).map((a) => {
    const j = a.jobs as unknown as { title: string; companies: { name: string } | null } | null;
    return { id: a.id, jobId: a.job_id, status: a.status, title: j?.title ?? "Role", company: j?.companies?.name ?? "" };
  });
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <h1 className="text-[28px] font-extrabold tracking-tight">Your applications</h1>
      {cards.length === 0 ? <p className="card-quest p-6 font-semibold">Save a role from your feed to start tracking.</p> : <Board cards={cards} />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`app/(app)/settings/actions.ts`:

```ts
"use server";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";

export async function deleteMyData() {
  const { supabase, user } = await requireUser();
  const admin = createAdminClient();
  for (const bucket of ["cvs", "tailored"]) {
    const { data: files } = await admin.storage.from(bucket).list(user.id, { limit: 1000 });
    if (files?.length) await admin.storage.from(bucket).remove(files.map((f) => `${user.id}/${f.name}`));
  }
  for (const t of ["cv_scores", "cvs", "matches", "applications", "xp_events", "badges", "confirmed_facts"]) {
    await supabase.from(t).delete().eq("user_id", user.id);
  }
  await supabase.from("profiles").update({
    year: null, field: null, location_pref: [], needs_visa: false, target_archetype: null, archetypes: [], skills: [],
    onboarded: false, xp: 0, level: 1, streak_days: 0, last_active_date: null,
  }).eq("user_id", user.id);
  redirect("/onboarding");
}
```
