## Phase F — Demo and ship

### Task 25: Demo persona, demo login, E2E run, deploy

**Files:**
- Create: `seed/demo/ayse.json`, `scripts/make-demo-cv.ts`, `scripts/create-demo-user.ts`, `scripts/reset-demo.ts`, `scripts/record-demo-fixtures.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`, `e2e/demo.spec.ts`
- Modify: `app/login/page.tsx`, `app/login/actions.ts` (demo sign-in), `package.json` (scripts)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above.
- Produces: `seed/demo/ayse-demir-cv.pdf`, `fixtures/ai/parseCV/<key>.json`, `fixtures/ai/judgeCvRules/demo-<sector>.json`; `signInDemo()` server action; a deployed Vercel URL.

- [ ] **Step 1: Fictional persona**

`seed/demo/ayse.json` — a fictional `ParsedCV` (no real person). It is designed so that some skills appear only in prose, which lets tailoring honestly surface them:

```json
{
  "name": "Ayşe Demir", "email": "ayse.demir.demo@example.com", "phone": "+44 7700 900123", "location": "London",
  "links": ["github.com/ayse-demo"], "summary": null,
  "education": [{ "id": "e0", "institution": "University College London", "degree": "BSc Economics", "dates": "2025–2028", "grade": "Predicted 2:1",
    "details": [{ "id": "e0-d0", "text": "Modules: Econometrics (Stata), Statistics, Microeconomics" }, { "id": "e0-d1", "text": "A-levels: Maths A*, Economics A, History A" }] }],
  "experience": [{ "id": "x0", "org": "Marlowe Café", "title": "Barista (part-time)", "dates": "2024–2025", "location": "London", "bullets": [
    { "id": "x0-b0", "text": "Served customers in a busy café" },
    { "id": "x0-b1", "text": "Trained 3 new staff on the till and opening routine" }] }],
  "projects": [{ "id": "p0", "org": "London Rent Tracker", "title": "Personal project", "dates": "2025", "location": "", "bullets": [
    { "id": "p0-b0", "text": "Used Python and SQL to collect and clean 1,200 rental listings" },
    { "id": "p0-b1", "text": "Built charts in Excel comparing rents across 12 boroughs" }] }],
  "leadership": [{ "id": "l0", "org": "UCL Finance Society", "title": "Events Officer", "dates": "2025–present", "location": "", "bullets": [
    { "id": "l0-b0", "text": "Organised weekly society events" },
    { "id": "l0-b1", "text": "Managed a £2,000 events budget" }] }],
  "skills": [{ "group": "Technical", "items": ["Python", "SQL", "Excel"] }, { "group": "Languages", "items": ["English (fluent)", "Turkish (native)"] }],
  "interests": "Running (London 10k, 2025), chess", "sectionOrder": ["education", "experience", "projects", "leadership", "skills", "interests"],
  "yearGuess": "2nd",
  "archetypes": [
    { "key": "data_analytics", "score": 88, "why": ["Python and SQL rental-data project", "Econometrics with Stata"] },
    { "key": "consulting", "score": 72, "why": ["Organised society events and managed a budget"] },
    { "key": "finance_banking", "score": 65, "why": ["Finance Society committee role"] }
  ]
}
```

The "Organised weekly society events" bullet is deliberately unquantified. It produces the demo's honest "Quick win" fix. "Stata" appears only in the modules line, so tailoring can surface it.

- [ ] **Step 2: Demo CV PDF + parse fixture**

`scripts/make-demo-cv.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { renderCvPdf } from "@/lib/pdf/cv-document";
import { toTailored } from "@/lib/verify/tailoring";
import { contentHash } from "@/lib/ingest/fingerprint";
import type { ParsedCV } from "@/lib/types";

const cv = JSON.parse(readFileSync("seed/demo/ayse.json", "utf8")) as ParsedCV;
const pdf = await renderCvPdf(toTailored(cv), cv);
writeFileSync("seed/demo/ayse-demir-cv.pdf", pdf);
const key = contentHash(Buffer.from(pdf).toString("base64")).slice(0, 12);
mkdirSync("fixtures/ai/parseCV", { recursive: true });
writeFileSync(`fixtures/ai/parseCV/${key}.json`, JSON.stringify(cv, null, 1));
console.log(`demo CV written; parse fixture key ${key}`);
```

Add `"demo:cv": "tsx scripts/make-demo-cv.ts"` to scripts and run `npm run demo:cv`. Re-run it whenever `ayse.json` changes. Uploading the generated PDF in fixture mode then returns the persona exactly.

- [ ] **Step 3: Judge fixtures for the persona**

`scripts/record-demo-fixtures.ts`:
- With a live key, it records real LLM verdicts.
- Without one, it writes a minimal hand-labelled set: every LLM rule `na`, plus `spelling_errors: []`. That set is still honest, because it carries no claims.

```ts
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { rulesFor } from "@/lib/cv-rules";
import { judgeCvRules } from "@/lib/ai/judgeCvRules";
import { aiMode } from "@/lib/env";
import type { Archetype, ParsedCV } from "@/lib/types";

const cv = JSON.parse(readFileSync("seed/demo/ayse.json", "utf8")) as ParsedCV;
const text = [cv.education, cv.experience, cv.projects, cv.leadership].flat().map((e) => JSON.stringify(e)).join("\n");
mkdirSync("fixtures/ai/judgeCvRules", { recursive: true });
for (const sector of ["data_analytics", "consulting", "finance_banking"] as Archetype[]) {
  const rules = rulesFor(sector);
  let out: unknown;
  if (aiMode() === "live") {
    const r = await judgeCvRules({ cv, text, rules });
    out = { verdicts: r.results.map((x) => ({ rule_id: x.ruleId, status: x.status, evidence_line: x.evidenceLine, fix: x.fix })), spelling_errors: r.spellingErrors ?? [] };
  } else {
    out = { verdicts: [], spelling_errors: [] };
  }
  writeFileSync(`fixtures/ai/judgeCvRules/demo-${sector}.json`, JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));
  console.log(`demo-${sector}: ${aiMode()}`);
}
```

Add `"demo:fixtures": "tsx --env-file=.env.local scripts/record-demo-fixtures.ts"` to scripts. Run it once you have an API key, set `AI_MODE=live` while recording, and commit the fixtures. Do the same for any other function you want to replay offline.

- [ ] **Step 4: Demo user + demo sign-in**

`scripts/create-demo-user.ts`:

```ts
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { appendFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { scriptClient } from "./db";

const db = scriptClient();
const email = process.env.DEMO_EMAIL || "demo@placed.test";
const password = process.env.DEMO_PASSWORD || randomBytes(18).toString("base64url");
const { error } = await db.auth.admin.createUser({ email, password, email_confirm: true, user_metadata: { full_name: "Ayşe Demir" } });
if (error && !/already/i.test(error.message)) throw error;
if (!process.env.DEMO_PASSWORD) appendFileSync(".env.local", `\nDEMO_EMAIL=${email}\nDEMO_PASSWORD=${password}\n`);
console.log(`demo user ready: ${email} (credentials in .env.local)`);
```

`scripts/reset-demo.ts`:

```ts
import { scriptClient } from "./db";

const db = scriptClient();
const { data } = await db.auth.admin.listUsers();
const u = data.users.find((x) => x.email === process.env.DEMO_EMAIL);
if (!u) throw new Error("demo user not found — run demo:user");
for (const t of ["cv_scores", "cvs", "matches", "applications", "xp_events", "badges", "confirmed_facts"]) await db.from(t).delete().eq("user_id", u.id);
for (const b of ["cvs", "tailored"]) {
  const { data: files } = await db.storage.from(b).list(u.id);
  if (files?.length) await db.storage.from(b).remove(files.map((f) => `${u.id}/${f.name}`));
}
await db.from("profiles").update({ year: null, field: null, location_pref: [], needs_visa: false, target_archetype: null, archetypes: [], skills: [], onboarded: false, xp: 0, level: 1, streak_days: 0, last_active_date: null }).eq("user_id", u.id);
console.log("demo user reset");
```

Add the scripts `"demo:user": "tsx --env-file=.env.local scripts/create-demo-user.ts"` and `"demo:reset": "tsx --env-file=.env.local scripts/reset-demo.ts"`.

Append to `app/login/actions.ts`:

```ts
export async function signInDemo() {
  if (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_MODE !== "1") return { error: "Demo mode is off." };
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: process.env.DEMO_EMAIL!, password: process.env.DEMO_PASSWORD! });
  if (error) return { error: error.message };
  redirect("/onboarding");
}
```

In `app/login/page.tsx`, under the Google button, add:

```tsx
{process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_MODE === "1" && (
  <Button variant="secondary" className="w-full" onClick={() => start(async () => { const r = await signInDemo(); if (r?.error) setError(r.error); })}>
    Try the demo as Ayşe (fictional student)
  </Button>
)}
```

and import `signInDemo`.

```ts
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

test("3-minute demo flow", async ({ page, context }) => {
  await page.goto("/login");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Try the demo/ }).click();
  await page.waitForURL("**/onboarding");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "2nd year" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Next" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Economics" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Next" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "London" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Let's go" }).click();
  await page.waitForURL("**/profile/build");
  await page.setInputFiles('input[name="cv"]', "seed/demo/ayse-demir-cv.pdf");
  await page.waitForURL("**/profile**", { timeout: 60_000 });
  await expect(page.getByRole("img", { name: /CV score \d+ out of 100/ })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText("Source:").first()).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: /See my internships/ }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("article").nth(9)).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole("article").first().getByRole("link", { name: "View" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Tailor my CV for this role/ }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText("Every claim traced to your original CV")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
  const [popup] = await Promise.all([context.waitForEvent("page"), page.getByRole("link", { name: /Apply on company site/ }).click()]);
  await popup.close();
  await page.bringToFront();
  await page.evaluate(() => document.dispatchEvent(new Event("visibilitychange")));
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Yes, I applied" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText(/\+\d+ XP/)).toBeVisible();
});
```

Run: `npx playwright install chromium && npm run demo:user && npm run demo:reset && npm run e2e`

- Pitch points:

## Execution notes — preflight rulings (2026-10-13)

A pre-execution scan found 23 conflicts between tasks, the spec and the plan code. Test intent is kept; code is fixed. Where the spec and plan disagree, the spec wins. Each ruling lists the tasks it amends.

- C1 (T8,10,25) wrap every tsx script body in async main() + catch/exit(1); no "type":"module" — tsx compiles CJS, top-level await crashes (verified) — cost if wrong: trivial.
- C2 (T16) assert RULES.length===161 + per-prefix counts (G37 GEN12 FCQ12 FIN14 QNT12 CON13 SWE17 DAT12 PRD9 MKT12 RES11); GEN sectors "all" — spec says 161, finance doc inherits GEN — cost: re-scoring shifts slightly.
- C3 (T16) DIMENSION_WEIGHTS from research docs' consolidated rows (SWE/DAT 20/25/15/30/10, PRD/MKT 15/30/25/20/10, RES 15/20/20/35/10, FIN 15/25/20/20/20, QNT 10/20/20/40/10, CON 15/35/20/15/15) — spec §6.3 says docs' values; T18 tests unaffected — cost: weights differ from plan text.
- C4 (T19) profile page retries scoreCv when cv && !score; on failure "Score my CV" button — T18 promise otherwise unbuilt — cost: one extra LLM call on profile load.
- C5 (T21) Explanation type lives in lib/scoring/requirements.ts; lib/ai/explainMatch re-exports — Global Constraint no client import of lib/ai — cost: none.
- C6 (T9,21,23,25) runAi gains optional fromFixture post-processor; explainMatch/tailorCV fixtures keyed by job extId and post-processed like live; T25 records only parseCV/judgeCvRules fixtures — else fixture mode crashes — cost: small API addition.
- C7 (T22) verifyTailoring fact corpus = content text only (no ids/archetypes/sectionOrder/yearGuess) + confirmedFacts; org/title whole-token; add tests "Served 65 customers"→block, title "Lead"→block — Never-cut anti-fabrication soundness — cost: possible false blocks (reverts, safe direction).
- C8 (T22/23) VerifyReport adds optional keywordCoverage{before,after,missing[]} + gapsStillOpen[], filled in runTailoring — spec §7.3 — cost: none.
- C9 (T18) redactText also strips UK postcodes, cv.location string, street-address lines in first 3 lines; add test — Global Constraint/G-37 — cost: may over-redact a line.
- C10 (T25) E2E asserts "Badge unlocked: First Shot" instead of /\+\d+ XP/ — strict-mode ambiguity — cost: none.
- C11 (T25) waitForURL(/\/profile(\?.*)?$/,{timeout:60000}); exact:true on Next/View; devIndicators:false in next.config — flaky selectors — cost: none.
- C12 (T25) ship hand-labelled demo judge fixture (one partial on quantification rule with evidence_line "Organised weekly society events", fix with real-number placeholder) — demo must show cited quick win — cost: fixture is curated, labelled as demo.
- C13/C23 tasks' code blocks are authoritative; File Structure + Interfaces text updated in plan notes only — doc drift — cost: none.
- C14 dedupe: export APPLY_XP from gamify/core (feed uses it); lib/db/reset.ts resetUserData(db,userId) (T24, reused T25); pure isImageOnly in lib/cv/image-only.ts (T15, reused T17); cvSkillSource accepts structural subset (T11, reused T23); coverageFor(job,cv,student) in lib/scoring/coverage.ts (introduce at T20/21, reuse); lib/text/regex.ts escapeRegex + EMAIL_RE/PHONE_RE (T5 creates, reused T7,15,18) — reviewers would flag duplication — cost: small deviations from plan file layout.
- C15 (T2,5,10,12) spec wins: cohort clashes kept + flagged; jobs.cohort_clash boolean default false; JobForScoring.cohortClash; scoreFit soft flag "Term-time cohort (e.g. Winter)"; run.test expects kept=2, tag x2, markDead(1,["1","4"]) — cost: a few clash jobs appear lower in feed.
- C16 (T10) /api/ingest supports optional ?batch=N (companies [N*10,N*10+10)); without it cursor/time-box — spec §4 — cost: none.
- C17 (T23) tailoring change list shows jdRequirement + source bullet text via sourceRefs; must/nice matched/total before→after — spec §8, never-cut transparency — cost: none.
- C18 (T20) feed location chips London/UK/Europe/Remote (?loc=) via classifyRegion — spec §9.5 — cost: none.
- C19 (T19) SECTOR_CAVEATS in lib/cv-rules/profiles.ts rendered under ring; fix shows offending line + why (statement) + rephrase; image-only banner links quiz — spec §6.4, RF2 — cost: none.
- C20 (T24) tracker "applied" computes fitLabel via coverageFor+scoreFit and passes fitLabel/jobTitle/deadline to awardXP — spec §10 — cost: extra query.
- C21 (T1) env test deletes AI_MODE; (T21,T23) prompts.test asserts SYSTEM_EXPLAIN/SYSTEM_TAILOR contain UNTRUSTED_JD_RULE — RF4 — cost: none.
- C22 (T23) Font.register a Unicode TTF (Noto Sans or Plus Jakarta Sans regular+bold) vendored in public/fonts, added to outputFileTracingIncludes — "Ayşe" must render — cost: ~300KB fonts.
